1	# command-ingress design
2	
3	> 日期：2026-05-09
4	> 状态：draft
5	> 直接合同：command-ingress-brainstorm.md
6	> 依赖：task（types 已确认）、connection（API 已确认）、frame（API 已确认）
7	
8	## 类型引用约定
9	
10	本 design 引用的 task 类型以 **task-real design**（`codestable/features/rewrite-task/task-real-design.md`）为最终真相源。task-real 尚未实施，以下映射标注了当前代码 vs task-real design 的差异。command-ingress 实施必须等 task-real 实施完成后才能开始。
11	
12	| 当前代码名 | task-real design 名 | 变更性质 | 来源 |
13	|---|---|---|---|
14	| `TaskSchedulingMode` | `ScheduleDriver` discriminated union | 替代 | task-real design §1.1 |
15	| `WaitCondition` | `ConditionTerm`（新增 `logicOperator`） | 扩展 | task-real design §1.1 |
16	| `WaitConditionStepConfig.condition` | `WaitConditionConfig.conditions` 数组 | 单→数组 | task-real design §1.2 |
17	| `WaitConditionStepConfig.timeoutMs: number` | `WaitConditionConfig.timeoutMs?: number` | 必选→可选 | task-real design §1.2 |
18	| `SendStepDefinition.sendConfig` | `TaskStepDefinition.config` | 统一字段名 | task-real design §1.2 |
19	| `WaitConditionStepDefinition.waitConfig` | `TaskStepDefinition.config` | 统一字段名 | task-real design §1.2 |
20	| `DelayStepDefinition.delayConfig` | `TaskStepDefinition.config` | 统一字段名 | task-real design §1.2 |
21	| `TaskDefinition.schedulingMode` | `TaskDefinition.schedule: ScheduleDriver` | 替代 | task-real design §1.2 |
22	| `TaskDefinition.triggerSource` | 移除 | 删除 | task-real design §1.2 |
23	| `TaskDefinition.targetId?` | 移到 `SendStepConfig.targetId` | 下沉 | task-real design §1.2 |
24	| **不存在** | `TaskDefinition.fieldVariations` | **新增** | task-real design §1.2 |
25	| **不存在** | `TaskStopCondition.exitCondition` | **新增** | task-real design §1.2 |
26	| `SendStepConfig.fieldValues` | `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues` | 重命名 | task-real design §1.2 |
27	| `SendStepConfig.targetId?` | `SendStepConfig.targetId: string` | 可选→必需 | task-real design §1.2 |
28	
29	## 1. 消费者链集成
30	
31	### 1.1 TransportEventConsumer 接口
32	
33	```typescript
34	// rewrite/src/runtime/consumer-chain.ts（新文件）
35	
36	export interface TransportEventConsumer {
37	  readonly id: string;
38	  consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult>;
39	}
40	
41	export interface ConsumerResult {
42	  readonly consumed: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[];
43	  readonly remaining: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[];
44	}
45	```
46	
47	### 1.2 routingTick 改造方案
48	
49	**现状**（routing-tick.ts:41-54）：硬编码 `dataEvents → receiveService.drainInputSource()`。
50	
51	**改动**：在 `RewriteWiredFeatures` 中新增有序消费者列表，routingTick 链式调用。
52	
53	```typescript
54	// feature-wiring.ts 改动
55	
56	export interface RewriteWiredFeatures {
57	  // ... 现有字段不变
58	  readonly eventConsumers: readonly TransportEventConsumer[];
59	}
60	```
61	
62	```typescript
63	// routing-tick.ts 改动——替换 dataEvents → receive 的硬编码
64	
65	// 改前（line 52-54）:
66	const source = new ConnectionToReceiveInputSource(dataEvents);
67	const receiveOutcome = await features.receiveService.drainInputSource(source);
68	
69	// 改后:
70	let remaining = [...dataEvents];
71	for (const consumer of features.eventConsumers) {
72	  const result = await consumer.consume(remaining);
73	  remaining = [...result.remaining];
74	}
75	// remaining 交给 receive 处理
76	if (remaining.length > 0) {
77	  const source = new ConnectionToReceiveInputSource(remaining);
78	  const receiveOutcome = await features.receiveService.drainInputSource(source);
79	  // ... 后续 matchInputs/fanOut 逻辑不变
80	}
81	```
82	
83	**改动量**：约 15 行替换 + 2 行接口新增。routingTick 不做任何领域判断。
84	
85	### 1.3 消费者注册顺序
86	
87	在 `wireFeatures` 中装配（feature-wiring.ts）：
88	
89	```typescript
90	// wireFeatures 末尾新增
91	const commandIngressConsumer = createCommandIngressConsumer(/* options */);
92	
93	return {
94	  // ... 现有字段
95	  eventConsumers: [commandIngressConsumer],  // command-ingress 先于 receive
96	};
97	```
98	
99	command-ingress 先消费（SCOE 协议事件），remaining 交给 receive。command-ingress 未安装时 `eventConsumers` 为空数组，行为不变。
100	
101	### 1.4 RoutingTickResult 扩展
102	
103	```typescript
104	export interface RoutingTickResult {
105	  // ... 现有字段不变
106	  readonly consumerConsumed: number;  // 新增：被 command-ingress 消费的事件数
107	}
108	```
109	
110	## 2. ProtocolAdapter 接口
111	
112	### 2.1 接口定义
113	
114	```typescript
115	// rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/protocol-adapter.ts
116	
117	export interface ProtocolAdapter extends TransportEventConsumer {
118	  readonly protocolId: string;
119	}
120	
121	export interface ParsedCommand {
122	  readonly commandId: string;
123	  readonly commandCode: string;
124	  readonly commandFunction: ScoeCommandFunction;
125	  readonly rawBytes: readonly number[];
126	  readonly resolvedParams: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
127	  readonly commandConfig: ScoeCommandConfig;
128	  readonly connectionId: string;
129	  readonly occurredAt: string;
130	}
131	```
132	
133	### 2.2 ScoeProtocolAdapter
134	
135	ScoeProtocolAdapter 实现 `ProtocolAdapter` 接口，内部封装 SCOE 协议解析。
136	
137	```typescript
138	// rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts
139	
140	export interface ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions {
141	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
142	  readonly commandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
143	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
144	}
145	
146	export function createScoeProtocolAdapter(
147	  options: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions
148	): ProtocolAdapter
149	```
150	
151	#### 2.2.1 consume 实现流程
152	
153	```
154	consume(events):
155	  consumed = []
156	  remaining = []
157	  for each event in events:
158	    if canHandle(event):
159	      parsed = parse(event)
160	      consumed.push(event)
161	      await dispatch(parsed)  // → handler
162	    else:
163	      remaining.push(event)
164	  return { consumed, remaining }
165	```
166	
167	#### 2.2.2 canHandle 逻辑（字节级）
168	
169	```
170	canHandle(event):
171	  1. event.kind === 'data' && event.bytes !== undefined
172	  2. bytes 长度 >= functionCodeOffset + 4
173	  3. bytes[functionCodeOffset] === hex(scoeIdentifier)
174	  4. bytes[functionCodeOffset + 2] === 0xAA && bytes[functionCodeOffset + 3] === 0xAA
175	  5. 如果 scoeFramesLoaded === false:
176	     只匹配指令码 01（LOAD_SATELLITE_ID），其他返回 false
177	```
178	
179	来源：`scoeFrame.ts:37-99` validateFunctionCode 逻辑。
180	
181	#### 2.2.3 parse 逻辑（字节级）
182	
183	```
184	parse(event):
185	  1. validateFunctionCode:
186	     - 提取 scoeIdentifier（第 1 字节）和 commandCode（第 2 字节）
187	     - 在 commandConfigs 中查找 code 匹配的命令
188	
189	  2. validateChecksums:
190	     - 遍历 command.checksums
191	     - 对每个 enabled 的 checksum:
192	       sum = 0; for i in offset..offset+length: sum += bytes[i]
193	       expected = sum % 256
194	       actual = bytes[checksumOffset]
195	       验证 expected === actual
196	
197	  3. extractAndResolveParams:
198	     - 遍历 command.params
199	     - hex = bytesToHex(bytes.slice(param.offset, param.offset + param.length))
200	     - 在 param.options 中找 receiveCode === hex 的选项
201	     - resolvedParams[param.id] = option.value
202	
203	  4. return ParsedCommand
204	```
205	
206	来源：`scoeFrame.ts:310-407`。
207	
208	## 3. 命令配置数据模型
209	
210	### 3.1 ScoeGlobalConfig（协议层）
211	
212	保留旧配置分离。字段映射自 `src/types/scoe/index.ts:97-130`。
213	
214	```typescript
215	// rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/types.ts
216	
217	export interface ScoeGlobalConfig {
218	  readonly scoeIdentifier: string;
219	  readonly tcpServerIp: string;
220	  readonly tcpServerPort: number;
221	  readonly tcpServerAutoConnect: boolean;
222	  readonly udpIpAddress: string;
223	  readonly udpPort: number;
224	  readonly udpTargetId: string;                    // 确认帧发送目标（connection UDP targetId）
225	  readonly messageIdentifierOffset: number;
226	  readonly sourceIdentifierOffset: number;
227	  readonly destinationIdentifierOffset: number;
228	  readonly modelIdOffset: number;
229	  readonly satelliteIdOffset: number;
230	  readonly functionCodeOffset: number;
231	  readonly successFrameId?: string;
232	}
233	```
234	
235	**不迁移** `highlightConfigs`（UI 展示配置，不在 core 层）。
236	
237	### 3.2 ScoeCommandConfig（命令层）
238	
239	```typescript
240	export interface ScoeCommandConfig {
241	  readonly id: string;
242	  readonly label: string;
243	  readonly code: string;                           // 指令码 hex
244	  readonly function: ScoeCommandFunction;
245	  readonly checksums: readonly ChecksumConfig[];
246	  readonly params?: readonly ScoeCommandParam[];
247	  readonly frameMappings?: readonly ScoeCommandFrameMapping[];
248	  readonly completionConditions?: readonly CompletionConditionConfig[];
249	  readonly completionTimeout?: number;
250	  readonly successFrameId?: string;                // 命令级覆盖
251	  readonly sendInterval?: number;
252	}
253	
254	export enum ScoeCommandFunction {
255	  LOAD_SATELLITE_ID = 'load_satellite_id',
256	  UNLOAD_SATELLITE_ID = 'unload_satellite_id',
257	  HEALTH_CHECK = 'health_check',
258	  LINK_CHECK = 'link_check',
259	  SEND_FRAME = 'send_frame',
260	  READ_FILE_AND_SEND = 'read_file_and_send',
261	}
262	```
263	
264	### 3.3 ChecksumConfig
265	
266	```typescript
267	export interface ChecksumConfig {
268	  readonly enabled: boolean;
269	  readonly offset: number;
270	  readonly length: number;
271	  readonly checksumOffset: number;
272	}
273	```
274	
275	### 3.4 ScoeCommandParam（参数定义）
276	
277	```typescript
278	export interface ScoeCommandParam {
279	  readonly id: string;
280	  readonly label: string;
281	  readonly value: string;
282	  readonly type: 'string' | 'number' | 'boolean';
283	  readonly offset: number;
284	  readonly length: number;
285	  readonly targetInstanceId?: string;
286	  readonly targetFieldId?: string;
287	  readonly options: readonly ScoeParamOption[];
288	}
289	
290	export interface ScoeParamOption {
291	  readonly label: string;
292	  readonly value: string;
293	  readonly receiveCode: string;
294	}
295	```
296	
297	### 3.5 ScoeCommandFrameMapping（帧映射层）
298	
299	替代旧系统完整内嵌的 `SendFrameInstance`，改为引用。
300	
301	```typescript
302	export interface ScoeCommandFrameMapping {
303	  readonly frameId: string;                        // 引用 frame feature 的帧定义
304	  readonly instanceId: string;                     // 用于 param.targetInstanceId 关联
305	  readonly label?: string;                         // 帧映射可读名称（迁移自旧 SendFrameInstance.label）
306	  readonly description?: string;                   // 备注说明（迁移自旧 SendFrameInstance.description）
307	  readonly targetId: string;                       // 发送目标（UDP targetId）
308	  readonly fieldMappings: readonly FieldMapping[];
309	}
310	
311	export interface FieldMapping {
312	  readonly fieldId: string;
313	  readonly source: 'fixed' | 'param';
314	  readonly fixedValue?: string | number;
315	  readonly paramId?: string;                       // 从 resolvedParams 取值
316	}
317	```
318	
319	**不自建帧列表**：只存引用关系（frameId + fieldMappings），帧定义 truth 在 frame feature。
320	
321	### 3.6 CompletionConditionConfig（完成条件）
322	
323	```typescript
324	export interface CompletionConditionConfig {
325	  readonly id: string;
326	  readonly label: string;
327	  readonly sourceFrameId: string;
328	  readonly sourceFieldId: string;
329	  readonly useParam: boolean;
330	  readonly targetParamId?: string;
331	  readonly targetFixedValue?: string;
332	  readonly operator?: ComparisonOperator;           // 从 shared 引用
333	  readonly options?: readonly CompletionConditionOption[];
334	}
335	
336	export interface CompletionConditionOption {
337	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
338	  readonly matchValue: string;
339	}
340	```
341	
342	### 3.7 卫星配置
343	
344	LOAD 命令加载的卫星配置，运行时持有。
345	
346	```typescript
347	export interface SatelliteConfig {
348	  readonly satelliteId: string;
349	  readonly messageIdentifier: string;
350	  readonly sourceIdentifier: string;
351	  readonly destinationIdentifier: string;
352	  readonly modelId: string;
353	  readonly commandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
354	}
355	```
356	
357	旧系统中卫星配置通过 `scoeStore.satelliteConfigs` 持有（`loadSatelliteId.ts:35` 查找）。新系统中作为 command-ingress 领域数据持有，不通过 store 暴露。
358	
359	## 4. Handler 分发
360	
361	### 4.1 签名
362	
363	```typescript
364	// rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/handler.ts
365	
366	export interface CommandContext {
367	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
368	  readonly sendService: SendService;               // 确认帧发送（§7.2）
369	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
370	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
371	  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;
372	  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;         // LINK_CHECK 读 receive selector
373	  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;
374	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
375	  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;
376	}
377	
378	export type CommandHandler = (
379	  command: ParsedCommand,
380	  ctx: CommandContext
381	) => Promise<CommandResult>;
382	
383	export interface CommandResult {
384	  readonly success: boolean;
385	  readonly error?: string;
386	  readonly taskId?: string;
387	}
388	```
389	
390	### 4.2 Handler 注册
391	
392	```typescript
393	const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([
394	  [ScoeCommandFunction.LOAD_SATELLITE_ID, handleLoadSatelliteId],
395	  [ScoeCommandFunction.UNLOAD_SATELLITE_ID, handleUnloadSatelliteId],
396	  [ScoeCommandFunction.HEALTH_CHECK, handleHealthCheck],
397	  [ScoeCommandFunction.LINK_CHECK, handleLinkCheck],
398	  [ScoeCommandFunction.SEND_FRAME, handleSendFrame],
399	  [ScoeCommandFunction.READ_FILE_AND_SEND, handleReadFileAndSend],
400	]);
401	```
402	
403	### 4.3 6 种 Handler 实现
404	
405	#### 4.3.1 handleLoadSatelliteId
406	
407	前置条件：`stateReader.loadedSatelliteId === ''`（未加载才能 LOAD）。
408	
409	```
410	handleLoadSatelliteId(command, ctx):
411	  1. 卫星 ID 从 command.rawBytes[satelliteIdOffset..] 提取
412	  2. 在 satelliteConfigs 中查找匹配配置
413	  3. 验证配置完整性（messageIdentifier/sourceIdentifier/destinationIdentifier/modelId/satelliteId）
414	  4. ctx.stateWriter.setLoaded(satelliteId, commandConfigs)
415	  5. ctx.stateWriter.updateStatus({ scoeFramesLoaded: true, loadedSatelliteId, satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0 })
416	  6. return { success: true }
417	```
418	
419	来源：`loadSatelliteId.ts:14-95`。
420	
421	#### 4.3.2 handleUnloadSatelliteId
422	
423	前置条件：`stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded === true`。
424	
425	```
426	handleUnloadSatelliteId(command, ctx):
427	  1. ctx.stateWriter.resetRuntimeState()
428	     - scoeFramesLoaded = false
429	     - loadedSatelliteId = ''
430	     - satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds = 0
431	     - commandReceiveCount = 0
432	     - commandSuccessCount = 0
433	     - commandErrorCount = 0
434	     - healthStatus = 'unknown'
435	     - linkTestResult = 'unknown'
436	     - receiveCommandSuccess = false
437	  2. return { success: true }
438	```
439	
440	来源：旧系统 UNLOAD 清除全部运行时状态。
441	
442	#### 4.3.3 handleHealthCheck
443	
444	```
445	handleHealthCheck(command, ctx):
446	  1. snapshot = ctx.connectionSnapshot()
447	  2. return { success: true }
448	  // HEALTH_CHECK 只读连接状态，旧系统返回健康状态快照
449	  // 实际响应通过 UDP 成功帧发送，不在 handler 返回值里
450	```
451	
452	#### 4.3.4 handleLinkCheck
453	
454	```
455	handleLinkCheck(command, ctx):
456	  1. snapshot = ctx.receiveSnapshot()
457	  2. return { success: true }
458	  // LINK_CHECK 只读接收同步状态，实际响应通过 UDP 成功帧
459	```
460	
461	#### 4.3.5 handleSendFrame
462	
463	前置条件：`stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded === true`。
464	
465	```
466	handleSendFrame(command, ctx):
467	  1. commandConfig = command.commandConfig
468	  2. frameMappings = commandConfig.frameMappings
469	  3. taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(command, ctx)
470	  4. instance = ctx.taskService.createTask(taskDef)
471	  5. ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId)
472	  6. 注册 onSettled 回调：成功 → sendAckFrame(command, ctx)
473	  7. ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount()
474	  8. return { success: true, taskId: instance.instanceId }
475	```
476	
477	详细 TaskDefinition 构造见 §5。
478	
479	#### 4.3.6 handleReadFileAndSend
480	
481	前置条件：`stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded === true`。
482	
483	```
484	handleReadFileAndSend(command, ctx):
485	  1. 读取文件内容（通过 ctx.platformFileReader）
486	  2. taskDef = buildReadFileAndSendTask(command, ctx, fileLines)
487	  3. instance = ctx.taskService.createTask(taskDef)
488	  4. ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId)
489	  5. 注册 onSettled 回调：成功 → sendAckFrame(command, ctx)
490	  6. ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount()
491	  7. return { success: true, taskId: instance.instanceId }
492	```
493	
494	## 5. TaskDefinition 翻译规则
495	
496	### 5.1 SEND_FRAME → TaskDefinition
497	
498	使用 `schedule: { kind: 'immediate' }`（顺序执行所有步骤）。
499	
500	```typescript
501	function buildSendFrameTask(
502	  command: ParsedCommand,
503	  ctx: CommandContext,
504	): TaskDefinition {
505	  const config = command.commandConfig;
506	  const steps: TaskStepDefinition[] = [];
507	
508	  // 每个帧映射 → 一个 send step
509	  for (const mapping of config.frameMappings ?? []) {
510	    const userFieldValues: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = {};
511	    for (const fm of mapping.fieldMappings) {
512	      if (fm.source === 'param' && fm.paramId) {
513	        userFieldValues[fm.fieldId] = command.resolvedParams[fm.paramId] ?? '';
514	      } else if (fm.fixedValue !== undefined) {
515	        userFieldValues[fm.fieldId] = fm.fixedValue;
516	      }
517	    }
518	
519	    steps.push({
520	      id: `send-${mapping.instanceId}`,
521	      kind: 'send',
522	      config: {
523	        frameId: mapping.frameId,
524	        targetId: mapping.targetId,
525	        userFieldValues,
526	      },
527	    });
528	
529	    // 发送间隔（帧间）→ intervalAfterMs
530	    if (config.sendInterval && config.sendInterval > 0) {
531	      steps.push({
532	        id: `delay-after-${mapping.instanceId}`,
533	        kind: 'delay',
534	        config: { durationMs: config.sendInterval },
535	      });
536	    }
537	  }
538	
539	  // 完成条件 → wait-condition step（支持多条件数组）
540	  const waitConditions = buildWaitConditions(command, config.completionConditions);
541	  if (waitConditions.length > 0) {
542	    steps.push({
543	      id: 'wait-completion',
544	      kind: 'wait-condition',
545	      config: {
546	        conditions: waitConditions,
547	        timeoutMs: config.completionTimeout ?? 5000,
548	        onTimeout: 'fail',
549	      },
550	    });
551	  }
552	
553	  return {
554	    id: `scoe-send-${command.commandId}`,
555	    name: `SCOE SEND_FRAME: ${config.label}`,
556	    schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
557	    steps,
558	    errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
559	  };
560	}
561	```
562	
563	### 5.2 READ_FILE_AND_SEND → TaskDefinition
564	
565	task-real design 已确认引入 `FieldVariation`（task-real design §1.1），方案 A 可行。
566	
567	```typescript
568	function buildReadFileAndSendTask(
569	  command: ParsedCommand,
570	  ctx: CommandContext,
571	  fileLines: readonly string[],
572	): TaskDefinition {
573	  const config = command.commandConfig;
574	  const mapping = config.frameMappings?.[0];  // READ_FILE_AND_SEND 通常只有一个帧映射
575	  if (!mapping) throw new Error('No frame mapping for READ_FILE_AND_SEND');
576	
577	  // 找到文件参数对应的 fieldId
578	  const fileParam = config.params?.find(p => p.value?.includes('.txt'));
579	  const fileFieldMapping = mapping.fieldMappings.find(
580	    fm => fm.source === 'param' && fm.paramId === fileParam?.id
581	  );
582	
583	  const waitConditions = buildWaitConditions(command, config.completionConditions);
584	  const steps: TaskStepDefinition[] = [];
585	
586	  steps.push({
587	    id: 'send-rfs',
588	    kind: 'send',
589	    config: {
590	      frameId: mapping.frameId,
591	      targetId: mapping.targetId,
592	      userFieldValues: {},  // 由 fieldVariations 按轮次覆盖
593	    },
594	  });
595	
596	  if (waitConditions.length > 0) {
597	    steps.push({
598	      id: 'wait-rfs',
599	      kind: 'wait-condition',
600	      config: {
601	        conditions: waitConditions,
602	        timeoutMs: config.completionTimeout ?? 5000,
603	        onTimeout: 'fail',
604	      },
605	    });
606	  }
607	
608	  return {
609	    id: `scoe-rfs-${command.commandId}`,
610	    name: `SCOE READ_FILE_AND_SEND: ${config.label}`,
611	    schedule: { kind: 'timer', intervalMs: config.sendInterval ?? 1000 },
612	    steps,
613	    fieldVariations: fileFieldMapping
614	      ? [{ fieldId: fileFieldMapping.fieldId, values: fileLines }]
615	      : [],
616	    stopCondition: { maxIterations: fileLines.length },
617	    errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
618	  };
619	}
620	```
621	
622	来源：task-real design §1.1 FieldVariation + §1.2 TaskDefinition.fieldVariations。旧系统 `readFileAndSend.ts` 的 FieldVariation 机制被 task-real 原生支持。
623	
624	### 5.3 完成条件 → ConditionTerm[]
625	
626	task-real design 将 `WaitConditionStepConfig.condition` 改为 `WaitConditionConfig.conditions` 数组，支持多条件 AND/OR 组合。所有完成条件可合并为单个 `conditions` 数组。
627	
628	```typescript
629	function buildWaitConditions(
630	  command: ParsedCommand,
631	  condConfigs?: readonly CompletionConditionConfig[],
632	): ConditionTerm[] {
633	  if (!condConfigs || condConfigs.length === 0) return [];
634	
635	  return condConfigs.map((condConfig) => {
636	    if (condConfig.useParam && condConfig.targetParamId) {
637	      // 参数化模式：从 resolvedParams 取值 → 查 param.options 索引 → 同索引取 condition.options
638	      const paramValue = command.resolvedParams[condConfig.targetParamId];
639	      const param = command.commandConfig.params?.find(p => p.id === condConfig.targetParamId);
640	      const optionIndex = param?.options.findIndex(o => o.value === paramValue) ?? -1;
641	      const condOption = condConfig.options?.[optionIndex];
642	
643	      return {
644	        frameId: condConfig.sourceFrameId,
645	        fieldId: condConfig.sourceFieldId,
646	        operator: condOption?.operator ?? 'eq' as ComparisonOperator,
647	        threshold: condOption?.matchValue ?? '',
648	      };
649	    } else {
650	      // 固定值模式
651	      return {
652	        frameId: condConfig.sourceFrameId,
653	        fieldId: condConfig.sourceFieldId,
654	        operator: condConfig.operator ?? 'eq' as ComparisonOperator,
655	        threshold: condConfig.targetFixedValue ?? '',
656	      };
657	    }
658	  });
659	}
660	```
661	
662	来源：`scoeFrame.ts:416-491` checkCompletionConditions 逻辑。task-real design §1.1 ConditionTerm 支持 `logicOperator`（默认 `'and'`），与旧系统"所有条件都满足"语义一致，无需显式设置。
663	
664	## 6. 参数 resolve 流程
665	
666	在 `ScoeProtocolAdapter.parse()` 内完成，resolve 为 literal 值后传入 handler。
667	
668	```
669	extractAndResolveParams(data, params):
670	  result = {}
671	  for param in params:
672	    bytes = data[param.offset ... param.offset + param.length]
673	    hex = bytesToUpperCaseHex(bytes)
674	
675	    matched = param.options.find(opt =>
676	      normalize(opt.receiveCode) === normalize(hex)
677	    )
678	    // normalize: 移除 0x 前缀、大写、左补零到 param.length*2 位
679	
680	    result[param.id] = matched?.value ?? hex  // 无匹配时保留原始 hex
681	  return result
682	```
683	
684	来源：`scoeFrame.ts:366-407`。
685	
686	**参数到帧字段的映射**：通过 `ScoeCommandFrameMapping.fieldMappings` 中的 `source: 'param'` + `paramId` 关联。handler 构造 TaskDefinition 时将 `resolvedParams[paramId]` 填入 `fieldValues[fieldId]`。
687	
688	旧系统通过 `param.targetInstanceId + param.targetFieldId` 指向帧实例字段。新系统中 `targetInstanceId` 对应 `ScoeCommandFrameMapping.instanceId`，`targetFieldId` 对应 `FieldMapping.fieldId`。迁移时直接映射。
689	
690	## 7. 确认帧发送
691	
692	### 7.1 策略
693	
694	- 成功：adapter 在 `taskService.onSettled(instanceId)` resolve 后，通过 `connectionService.write()` 发送 `successFrameId` 帧（UDP）
695	- 失败/超时：只更新领域状态（errorCount++、lastErrorReason），不发任何回应帧
696	
697	### 7.2 发送流程
698	
699	**选定路径**：通过 `sendService.execute()` 发送确认帧。sendService 已有 frame→bytes 序列化 + connection write 能力，command-ingress 不需要自行序列化帧。
700	
701	```typescript
702	async function sendAckFrame(
703	  command: ParsedCommand,
704	  ctx: CommandContext,
705	): Promise<void> {
706	  const successFrameId = command.commandConfig.successFrameId
707	    ?? ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().successFrameId;
708	  if (!successFrameId) return;
709	
710	  const frame = ctx.frameReader.getFrame(successFrameId);
711	  if (!frame) return;
712	
713	  // 通过 sendService 发送：序列化帧 + 写入 connection（复用 send service 的 frameToBuffer）
714	  await ctx.sendService.execute({
715	    frameId: successFrameId,
716	    targetId: command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId  // UDP targetId
717	              ?? ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,
718	    userFieldValues: {},
719	  });
720	}
721	```
722	
723	**前提**：CommandContext 需新增 `sendService: SendService` 字段。sendService.execute() 的 `SendRequest` 接口已在 send feature 中定义，接受 `{ frameId, targetId, fieldValues }`。
724	
725	**GAP 关闭**：G3 已解决，确认帧发送路径锁定为 sendService。
726	
727	### 7.3 onSettled 注册方式
728	
729	```typescript
730	// 在 handler 中
731	ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
732	
733	// fire-and-forget：监听 settled 后发确认帧
734	ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(() => {
735	  const inst = ctx.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
736	  if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
737	    sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
738	    ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
739	  } else {
740	    ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(
741	      inst?.error ?? 'task failed'
742	    );
743	  }
744	});
745	```
746	
747	**注意**：`TaskService.onSettled()` 返回 `Promise<void>`，resolve 时 task 已进入 terminal 状态。通过 `getInstance()` 检查 `lifecycle` 判断成功/失败。来源：task-service.ts:235-242。
748	
749	## 8. 领域状态
750	
751	### 8.1 CommandIngressState
752	
753	```typescript
754	// rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/state.ts
755	
756	export interface CommandIngressState {
757	  // 统计计数（运行时内存，不持久化）
758	  readonly commandReceiveCount: number;
759	  readonly commandSuccessCount: number;
760	  readonly commandErrorCount: number;
761	
762	  // 运行时状态
763	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
764	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
765	  readonly healthStatus: 'unknown' | 'healthy' | 'error';
766	  readonly linkTestResult: 'unknown' | 'pass' | 'fail';
767	  readonly lastCommandCode: string;
768	  readonly receiveCommandSuccess: boolean;
769	
770	  // 计时器
771	  readonly runtimeSeconds: number;
772	  readonly satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: number;
773	  readonly lastErrorReason: string;
774	
775	  // 配置
776	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
777	  readonly activeCommandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
778	}
779	```
780	
781	来源：`src/types/scoe/index.ts:57-82` ScoeStatus 的 12 个字段。
782	
783	### 8.2 两阶段状态机
784	
785	**阶段 1：scoeFramesLoaded = false**
786	
787	- ScoeProtocolAdapter.canHandle() 只匹配指令码 01（LOAD_SATELLITE_ID）
788	- 其他所有 SCOE 事件返回 `canHandle: false`，流入 receive
789	
790	**阶段 2：scoeFramesLoaded = true**
791	
792	- LOAD 成功后切换到阶段 2
793	- 所有 6 种命令可识别和处理
794	- handler 加前置条件检查（UNLOAD 将状态切回阶段 1）
795	
796	来源：`scoeFrame.ts:191-213`。
797	
798	### 8.3 每秒状态机轮询
799	
800	```typescript
801	// rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/services/status-timer.ts
802	
803	function startStatusTimer(
804	  stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter,
805	  intervalMs: number = 1000,
806	): () => void {
807	  return setInterval(() => {
808	    stateWriter.tickSecond();
809	  }, intervalMs);
810	}
811	
812	// CommandIngressStateWriter.tickSecond():
813	//   runtimeSeconds++
814	//   receiveCommandSuccess = false
815	//   if (!scoeFramesLoaded):
816	//     清空所有运行时统计（receiveCount/successCount/errorCount/healthStatus/linkTestResult）
817	//   else:
818	//     satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds++
819	```
820	
821	**有意不迁移旧行为**：旧 `scoeStore.ts:293-311` statusUpdate 还包含 `checkSatelliteLoad()` 和 `sendScoeFrames()` 两步。分析：
822	- `checkSatelliteLoad()`：旧系统在 store 层做卫星配置完整性检查，新系统中 LOAD handler 已内含验证逻辑（§4.3.1 步骤 3），无需在定时器重复检查。
823	- `sendScoeFrames()`：旧系统在定时器中触发帧发送，新系统中帧发送由 handler 通过 TaskDefinition 驱动，不依赖定时器触发。
824	
825	来源：`scoeStore.ts:293-311` statusUpdate 定时器。
826	
827	### 8.4 Selector 清单
828	
829	```typescript
830	// rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/selectors/
831	
832	export function selectCommandIngressSnapshot(state: CommandIngressState): CommandIngressSnapshot;
833	export function selectScoeStatistics(state: CommandIngressState): ScoeStatisticsSnapshot;
834	export function selectScoeRuntimeStatus(state: CommandIngressState): ScoeRuntimeStatus;
835	export function selectScoeFramesLoaded(state: CommandIngressState): boolean;
836	export function selectLoadedSatelliteId(state: CommandIngressState): string;
837	```
838	
839	所有 selector 返回 `readonly` 深拷贝快照，满足 Selector 不可变约束（CLAUDE.md）。
840	
841	### 8.5 State Reader/Writer 分离
842	
843	```typescript
844	export interface CommandIngressStateReader {
845	  getSnapshot(): CommandIngressState;
846	  globalConfig(): ScoeGlobalConfig;
847	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
848	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
849	}
850	
851	export interface CommandIngressStateWriter {
852	  setLoaded(satelliteId: string, configs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[]): void;
853	  resetRuntimeState(): void;
854	  updateStatus(patch: Partial<CommandIngressState>): void;
855	  incrementReceiveCount(): void;
856	  incrementSuccessCount(): void;
857	  incrementErrorCount(reason: string): void;
858	  tickSecond(): void;
859	}
860	```
861	
862	## 9. 迁移计划
863	
864	### 9.1 字段映射表：ScoeGlobalConfig（14 字段）
865	
866	| 旧字段 | 旧类型 | 新字段 | 新类型 | 备注 |
867	|---|---|---|---|---|
868	| scoeIdentifier | string | scoeIdentifier | string | 直迁 |
869	| tcpServerIp | string | tcpServerIp | string | 直迁 |
870	| tcpServerPort | number | tcpServerPort | number | 直迁 |
871	| tcpServerAutoConnect | boolean | tcpServerAutoConnect | boolean | 直迁 |
872	| udpIpAddress | string | udpIpAddress | string | 直迁 |
873	| udpPort | number | udpPort | number | 直迁 |
874	| — | — | udpTargetId | string | **新增**：旧系统硬编码 `'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote'`，新系统改为配置 |
875	| messageIdentifierOffset | number | messageIdentifierOffset | number | 直迁 |
876	| sourceIdentifierOffset | number | sourceIdentifierOffset | number | 直迁 |
877	| destinationIdentifierOffset | number | destinationIdentifierOffset | number | 直迁 |
878	| modelIdOffset | number | modelIdOffset | number | 直迁 |
879	| satelliteIdOffset | number | satelliteIdOffset | number | 直迁 |
880	| functionCodeOffset | number | functionCodeOffset | number | 直迁 |
881	| successFrameId? | string | successFrameId? | string | 直迁 |
882	| highlightConfigs? | HighlightConfigs | — | — | 不迁移，UI 层 |
883	
884	### 9.2 字段映射表：ScoeReceiveCommand（11 字段）
885	
886	| 旧字段 | 旧类型 | 新字段 | 新类型 | 备注 |
887	|---|---|---|---|---|
888	| id | string | id | string | 直迁 |
889	| label | string | label | string | 直迁 |
890	| code | string | code | string | 直迁 |
891	| sendInterval? | number | sendInterval? | number | 直迁 |
892	| params? | ScoeCommandParams[] | params? | ScoeCommandParam[] | 字段微调（见 9.3） |
893	| function | ScoeCommandFunction | function | ScoeCommandFunction | 枚举值不变 |
894	| checksums | ChecksumConfig[] | checksums | ChecksumConfig[] | 直迁 |
895	| frameInstances? | SendFrameInstance[] | frameMappings? | ScoeCommandFrameMapping[] | **结构性变化**（见 9.4） |
896	| completionConditions? | CompletionCondition[] | completionConditions? | CompletionConditionConfig[] | operator 类型变化（见 9.5） |
897	| completionTimeout? | number | completionTimeout? | number | 直迁 |
898	| successFrameId? | string | successFrameId? | string | 直迁 |
899	
900	### 9.3 ScoeCommandParams → ScoeCommandParam
901	
902	| 旧字段 | 新字段 | 备注 |
903	|---|---|---|
904	| id | id | 直迁 |
905	| label | label | 直迁 |
906	| value | value | 直迁 |
907	| type | type | 直迁 |
908	| offset | offset | 直迁 |
909	| length | length | 直迁 |
910	| targetInstanceId | targetInstanceId | 语义变化：从帧实例 ID 变为 ScoeCommandFrameMapping.instanceId |
911	| targetFieldId | targetFieldId | 语义变化：从帧字段 ID 变为 FieldMapping.fieldId |
912	| options | options | 内部结构不变（label/value/receiveCode） |
913	
914	### 9.4 SendFrameInstance → ScoeCommandFrameMapping
915	
916	旧系统完整内嵌 SendFrameInstance（含完整字段数组、expressionConfig、strategyConfig）。新系统改为轻量引用。
917	
918	迁移步骤：
919	1. 从旧 `frameInstances[].frameId` 提取 `frameId`
920	2. 从旧 `frameInstances[].id` 生成 `instanceId`（用于 param.targetInstanceId 关联）
921	3. 从旧 `frameInstances[].targetId` 或默认 `'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote'` 生成 `targetId`
922	4. 从旧 `frameInstances[].fields[]` 中提取有 `targetInstanceId` 匹配的字段 → 生成 `fieldMappings`
923	5. 从旧 `frameInstances[].fields[].value` 提取固定值 → `source: 'fixed', fixedValue`
924	6. 从旧 `frameInstances[].fields[]` 中被 param 引用的字段 → `source: 'param', paramId`
925	
926	**不迁移**：
927	- `label`：迁移到 `ScoeCommandFrameMapping.label?`
928	- `description`：迁移到 `ScoeCommandFrameMapping.description?`
929	- `expressionConfig`：旧表达式引擎 store 依赖，新系统用 shared/ 表达式引擎替代，但 SCOE 发帧场景不需要表达式
930	- `strategyConfig`：发送策略由 TaskDefinition.schedulingMode 和 step 定义替代
931	- `sendCount / lastSentAt`：运行时统计，不持久化
932	- `isFavorite / isSCOEFrame / paramCount`：UI 辅助字段
933	- `createdAt / updatedAt`：运行时元数据，不持久化
934	
935	**SendInstanceField 大部分字段不需要迁移到 ScoeCommandFrameMapping**：`label`、`dataType`、`inputType`、`factor`、`length`、`bigEndian`、`isASCII`、`validOption`、`options`、`description`、`configurable`、`dataParticipationType` 属于帧定义元数据，在新系统中由 frame feature 持有。command-ingress 只存 `fieldId` + 映射关系（fixedValue 或 paramId），帧元数据通过 `frameId` 引用从 frame feature 获取。迁移时 `fields[].id` → `FieldMapping.fieldId`。
936	
937	### 9.5 CompletionCondition → CompletionConditionConfig
938	
939	| 旧字段 | 新字段 | 备注 |
940	|---|---|---|
941	| id | id | 直迁 |
942	| label | label | 直迁 |
943	| sourceFrameId | sourceFrameId | 直迁 |
944	| sourceFieldId | sourceFieldId | 直迁 |
945	| useParam | useParam | 直迁 |
946	| targetParamId? | targetParamId? | 直迁 |
947	| targetFixedValue? | targetFixedValue? | 直迁 |
948	| operator? | operator? | 类型从旧 `MatchOperator`（string 枚举如 `'equal'`）映射到 `ComparisonOperator`（`'eq'`） |
949	| options? | options? | 内部 operator 同理映射 |
950	
951	**operator 映射**：
952	
953	| 旧 MatchOperator | 新 ComparisonOperator |
954	|---|---|
955	| `'equal'` | `'eq'` |
956	| `'not_equal'` | `'neq'` |
957	| `'greater_than'` | `'gt'` |
958	| `'less_than'` | `'lt'` |
959	| `'greater_equal'` | `'gte'` |
960	| `'less_equal'` | `'lte'` |
961	| `'contains'` | `'contains'` |
962	
963	### 9.6 迁移脚本
964	
965	编写一次性 TypeScript 脚本，输入：
966	- `scoeReceiveCommands.json` → 输出 `scoe-command-configs.json`（ScoeCommandConfig[]）
967	- `scoeGlobalConfig` 来源（旧 scoeStore 或独立 JSON）→ 输出 `scoe-global-config.json`
968	
969	脚本放在 `rewrite/scripts/migrate-scoe-config.ts`，不在 feature 目录内。
970

1020	
1021	## 12. 连接断开清理
1022	
1023	brainstorm 决策 6：监听 connection disconnect event，取消与该连接关联的正在执行的 task。
1024	
1025	### 12.1 监听方式
1026	
1027	在 `command-ingress-service` 初始化时，通过 connection event 轮询机制检测断开。由于 connection 只有拉取 API（无订阅），在 routingTick 的消费者链流程中检测：
1028	
1029	```typescript
1030	// command-ingress consumer.consume() 内部
1031	// 每次被调用时检查 connection 状态变化
1032	// 记录已知的 activeConnectionIds
1033	// 如果某个 connectionId 不再出现在 events 中且不在 connectionFacts 中，视为断开
1034	```
1035	
1036	或者通过 `connectionService.listConnectionFacts()` 在每次 tick 时检查已连接的 target 列表变化。
1037	
1038	### 12.2 取消关联 task
1039	
1040	```typescript
1041	// 当检测到 TCP server 客户端断开时
1042	// 查找该 connectionId 关联的正在运行的 task instances
1043	// 调用 taskService.stopTask(instanceId) 取消
1044	// 清理内部 tracking map（connectionId → taskInstanceIds）
1045	```
1046	
1047	### 12.3 Tracking Map
1048	
1049	command-ingress 维护 `Map<connectionId, Set<taskInstanceId>>` 记录哪些 task 是由哪个连接的命令创建的。handler 创建 task 时记录关联，onSettled resolve 后清理。
1050	
1051	来源：brainstorm 决策 6 + R3 反向验证（连接断开清理，中等严重性）。
1052

```
1	import type { ProtocolAdapter, ParsedCommand } from '../core';
2	import type {
3	  ScoeGlobalConfig,
4	  ScoeCommandConfig,
5	  ChecksumConfig,
6	  ScoeCommandParam,
7	} from '../core';
8	import type { CommandIngressStateReader } from '../core';
9	import type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
10	import type { ConsumerResult } from '@/runtime/consumer-chain';
11	
12	export interface ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions {
13	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
14	  readonly commandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
15	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
16	}
17	
18	export function createScoeProtocolAdapter(
19	  options: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions,
20	): ProtocolAdapter {
21	  const { globalConfig, stateReader } = options;
22	  const commandConfigs = [...options.commandConfigs];
23	
24	  function canHandle(event: TransportEventSnapshot): boolean {
25	    if (event.kind !== 'data' || event.bytes === undefined) return false;
26	
27	    const bytes = event.bytes;
28	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
29	
30	    if (bytes.length < fcOffset + 4) return false;
31	
32	    const identifierByte = bytes[fcOffset];
33	    const expectedIdentifier = hexToByte(globalConfig.scoeIdentifier);
34	    if (expectedIdentifier === null || identifierByte !== expectedIdentifier) return false;
35	
36	    // Fixed 0xAA 0xAA at offset +2 and +3
37	    if (bytes[fcOffset + 2] !== 0xaa || bytes[fcOffset + 3] !== 0xaa) return false;
38	
39	    // Two-phase state machine
40	    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
41	      // Phase 1: only match LOAD_SATELLITE_ID (command code 01)
42	      const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]!);
43	      if (commandCode !== '01') return false;
44	    }
45	
46	    return true;
47	  }
48	
49	  function parse(event: TransportEventSnapshot): ParsedCommand {
50	    if (event.bytes === undefined) throw new Error('Event has no bytes');
51	
52	    const bytes = event.bytes;
53	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
54	
55	    const codeByte = bytes[fcOffset + 1];
56	    if (codeByte === undefined) throw new Error('Command code byte missing');
57	
58	    const commandCode = byteToHex(codeByte);
59	    const commandConfig = commandConfigs.find((c) => c.code === commandCode);
60	    if (!commandConfig) {
61	      throw new Error(`Unknown SCOE command code: ${commandCode}`);
62	    }
63	
64	    validateChecksums(bytes, commandConfig.checksums);
65	
66	    const resolvedParams = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, commandConfig.params ?? []);
67	
68	    return {
69	      commandId: `scoe-cmd-${event.id}`,
70	      commandCode,
71	      commandFunction: commandConfig.function,
72	      rawBytes: [...bytes],
73	      resolvedParams,
74	      commandConfig,
75	      connectionId: event.connectionId,
76	      occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
77	    };
78	  }
79	
80	  return {
81	    id: 'scoe-protocol',
82	    protocolId: 'scoe-tcp',
83	
84	    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {
85	      const consumed: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
86	      const remaining: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
87	
88	      for (const event of events) {
89	        if (canHandle(event)) {
90	          try {
91	            // W1: parse validates frame integrity (checksum, function code, params).
92	            // Dispatch to handlers will be added in W2. Parsed result is intentionally
93	            // not stored here — consume() only categorizes events for the consumer chain.
94	            parse(event);
95	          } catch (err) {
96	            // Parse failure (checksum, unknown command) — still consume the event
97	            // to prevent it from falling through to receive
98	            console.warn(`SCOE parse error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
99	          }
100	          consumed.push(event);
101	        } else {
102	          remaining.push(event);
103	        }
104	      }
105	
106	      return { consumed, remaining };
107	    },
108	  };
109	}
110	
111	// --- Byte-level helpers (exported for testing) ---
112	
113	export function byteToHex(byte: number): string {
114	  return byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase();
115	}
116	
117	export function hexToByte(hex: string): number | null {
118	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '');
119	  const value = parseInt(cleaned, 16);
120	  return Number.isNaN(value) ? null : value;
121	}
122	
123	export function validateChecksums(
124	  bytes: readonly number[],
125	  checksums: readonly ChecksumConfig[],
126	): void {
127	  for (const cs of checksums) {
128	    if (!cs.enabled) continue;
129	
130	    let sum = 0;
131	    for (let i = cs.offset; i < cs.offset + cs.length; i++) {
132	      sum += bytes[i] ?? 0;
133	    }
134	    const expected = sum % 256;
135	    const actual = bytes[cs.checksumOffset] ?? 0;
136	
137	    if (expected !== actual) {
138	      throw new Error(
139	        `Checksum failed at offset ${cs.checksumOffset}: expected ${expected}, got ${actual}`,
140	      );
141	    }
142	  }
143	}
144	
145	export function extractAndResolveParams(
146	  bytes: readonly number[],
147	  params: readonly ScoeCommandParam[],
148	): Record<string, string> {
149	  const result: Record<string, string> = {};
150	
151	  for (const param of params) {
152	    const slice = bytes.slice(param.offset, param.offset + param.length);
153	    const hex = bytesToUpperCaseHex(slice);
154	    const normalized = normalizeHex(hex, param.length * 2);
155	
156	    const matched = param.options.find(
157	      (opt) => normalizeHex(opt.receiveCode, param.length * 2) === normalized,
158	    );
159	
160	    result[param.id] = matched?.value ?? hex;
161	  }
162	
163	  return result;
164	}
165	
166	function bytesToUpperCaseHex(bytes: readonly number[]): string {
167	  return Array.from(bytes)
168	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase())
169	    .join('');
170	}
171	
172	function normalizeHex(hex: string, targetLength: number): string {
173	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '').toUpperCase();
174	  return cleaned.padStart(targetLength, '0');
175	}
176
```

```
4	// --- Step kinds ---
5	
6	export const TASK_STEP_KINDS = ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay'] as const;
7	export type TaskStepKind = (typeof TASK_STEP_KINDS)[number];
8	
9	// --- Comparison ---
10	
11	export const COMPARISON_OPERATORS = [
12	  'eq',
13	  'neq',
14	  'gt',
15	  'lt',
16	  'gte',
17	  'lte',
18	  'contains',
19	  'change',
20	  'any',
21	] as const;
22	export type ComparisonOperator = (typeof COMPARISON_OPERATORS)[number];
23	
24	// --- ConditionTerm (replaces WaitCondition) ---
25	
26	export interface ConditionTerm {
27	  readonly frameId: string;
28	  readonly fieldId: string;
29	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
30	  readonly threshold: string | number;
31	  readonly sourceId?: string;
32	  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or';
33	}
34	
35	// --- ScheduleDriver (replaces TaskSchedulingMode + flat scheduling fields) ---
36	
37	export type ScheduleDriver =
38	  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
39	  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
40	  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
41	
42	// --- FieldVariation ---
43	
44	export interface FieldVariation {
45	  readonly fieldId: string;
46	  readonly values: readonly (string | number)[];
47	}
48	
49	// --- StepRepeat ---
50	
51	export interface StepRepeat {
52	  readonly intervalMs: number;
53	  readonly until?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
54	  readonly maxCount?: number;
55	}
56	
57	// --- Step configs ---
58	
59	export interface SendStepConfig {
60	  readonly frameId: string;
61	  readonly targetId: string;
62	  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
63	  readonly variables?: ReadonlyMap<string, string | number | boolean>;
64	  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;
65	  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;
66	}
67	
68	export interface WaitConditionConfig {
69	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];
70	  readonly timeoutMs?: number;
71	  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
72	}
73	
74	export interface DelayStepConfig {
75	  readonly durationMs: number;
76	}
77	
78	// --- Step definitions ---
79	
80	export interface SendStepDefinition {
81	  readonly id: string;
82	  readonly name?: string;
83	  readonly kind: 'send';
84	  readonly config: SendStepConfig;
85	}
86	
87	export interface WaitConditionStepDefinition {
88	  readonly id: string;
89	  readonly name?: string;
90	  readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
91	  readonly config: WaitConditionConfig;
92	}
93	
94	export interface DelayStepDefinition {
95	  readonly id: string;
96	  readonly name?: string;
97	  readonly kind: 'delay';
98	  readonly config: DelayStepConfig;
99	}
100	
101	export type TaskStepDefinition =
102	  | SendStepDefinition
103	  | WaitConditionStepDefinition
104	  | DelayStepDefinition;
105	
106	// --- Error policy ---
107	
108	export const TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS = ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause'] as const;
109	export type TaskErrorAction = (typeof TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS)[number];
110	
111	export interface TaskErrorPolicy {
112	  readonly onFailure: TaskErrorAction;
113	  readonly retryCount?: number;
114	  readonly retryDelayMs?: number;
115	}
116	
117	// --- Stop condition ---
118	
119	export interface TaskStopCondition {
120	  readonly maxIterations?: number;
121	  readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
122	  readonly exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
123	}
124	
125	// --- TaskDefinition ---
126	
127	export interface TaskDefinition {
128	  readonly id: string;
129	  readonly name: string;
130	  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
131	  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver;
132	  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
133	  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];
134	  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
135	}
136	
137	// --- ResolvedStopCondition + helper ---
138	
139	export type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
140	
141	export function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
142	  if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
143	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
144	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
145	  }
146	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};
147	}
148	
149	// --- Lifecycle ---
150	
151	export const TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = [
152	  'created',
153	  'running',
154	  'paused',
155	  'stopped',
156	  'completed',
157	  'failed',
158	] as const;
159	export type TaskLifecycleStatus = (typeof TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES)[number];
160	
161	export type LifecycleAction = 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail';
162	
163	// --- Step results ---
164	
165	export interface SendStepResult {
166	  readonly kind: 'send';
167	  readonly sendResult: SendResult;
168	}
169	
170	export interface WaitConditionStepResult {
171	  readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
172	  readonly matched: boolean;
173	  readonly matchedValue?: number | string;
174	  readonly timedOut: boolean;
175	}
176	
177	export interface DelayStepResult {
178	  readonly kind: 'delay';
179	  readonly completed: boolean;
180	}
181	
182	export type TaskStepResultPayload =
183	  | SendStepResult
184	  | WaitConditionStepResult
185	  | DelayStepResult;
186	
187	export type TaskStepResult =
188	  | (SendStepResult & { readonly stepIndex: number; readonly iteration: number; readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction })
189	  | (WaitConditionStepResult & { readonly stepIndex: number; readonly iteration: number; readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction })
190	  | (DelayStepResult & { readonly stepIndex: number; readonly iteration: number; readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction });
191	
192	// --- Instance state ---
193	
194	export interface TaskInstanceState {
195	  readonly instanceId: string;
196	  readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition;
197	  readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus;
198	  readonly startedAt?: string;
199	  readonly pausedAt?: string;
200	  readonly stoppedAt?: string;
201	  readonly completedAt?: string;
202	  readonly failedAt?: string;
203	  readonly currentStepIndex: number;
204	  readonly currentIteration: number;
205	  readonly stepResults: readonly TaskStepResult[];
206	  readonly error?: string;
207	}
208	
209	export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
210	
211	// --- Progress ---
212	
213	export interface TaskProgress {
214	  readonly stepsTotal: number;
215	  readonly stepsCompleted: number;
216	  readonly stepsFailed: number;
217	  readonly stepsSkipped: number;
218	  readonly iterationsCompleted: number;
219	  readonly iterationsTotal: number | null;
220	  readonly elapsedMs: number;
221	  readonly estimatedRemainingMs: number | null;
222	  readonly lastStepResult: TaskStepResult | null;
223	}
224	
225	// --- Execution summary ---
226	
227	export interface TaskExecutionSummary {
228	  readonly kind: 'completed' | 'stopped' | 'failed';
229	  readonly summary: {
230	    readonly stepsTotal: number;
231	    readonly stepsSucceeded: number;
232	    readonly stepsFailed: number;
233	    readonly stepsSkipped: number;
234	  };
235	  readonly durationMs: number;
236	  readonly startedAt: string;
237	  readonly finishedAt: string;
238	}
239	
240	// --- Condition match input ---
241	
242	export interface ConditionMatchInput {
243	  readonly frameId: string;
244	  readonly sourceId?: string;
245	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
246	}
```

```
1	import type { ScoeGlobalConfig, ScoeCommandConfig, HealthStatus, LinkTestResult } from './types';
2	
3	// --- State ---
4	
5	export interface CommandIngressState {
6	  readonly commandReceiveCount: number;
7	  readonly commandSuccessCount: number;
8	  readonly commandErrorCount: number;
9	
10	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
11	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
12	  readonly healthStatus: HealthStatus;
13	  readonly linkTestResult: LinkTestResult;
14	  readonly lastCommandCode: string;
15	  readonly receiveCommandSuccess: boolean;
16	
17	  readonly runtimeSeconds: number;
18	  readonly satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: number;
19	  readonly lastErrorReason: string;
20	
21	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
22	  readonly activeCommandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
23	}
24	
25	// --- Reader ---
26	
27	export interface CommandIngressStateReader {
28	  getSnapshot(): CommandIngressState;
29	  globalConfig(): ScoeGlobalConfig;
30	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
31	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
32	}
33	
34	// --- Writer ---
35	
36	export interface CommandIngressStateWriter {
37	  setLoaded(satelliteId: string, configs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[]): void;
38	  resetRuntimeState(): void;
39	  updateStatus(patch: Partial<Omit<CommandIngressState, 'globalConfig' | 'activeCommandConfigs'>>): void;
40	  incrementReceiveCount(): void;
41	  incrementSuccessCount(): void;
42	  incrementErrorCount(reason: string): void;
43	  tickSecond(): void;
44	}
45	
46	// --- Factory ---
47	
48	export interface CommandIngressStateContainer {
49	  readonly reader: CommandIngressStateReader;
50	  readonly writer: CommandIngressStateWriter;
51	}
52	
53	export function createCommandIngressState(
54	  globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig,
55	): CommandIngressStateContainer {
56	  let state: CommandIngressState = {
57	    commandReceiveCount: 0,
58	    commandSuccessCount: 0,
59	    commandErrorCount: 0,
60	    loadedSatelliteId: '',
61	    scoeFramesLoaded: false,
62	    healthStatus: 'unknown',
63	    linkTestResult: 'unknown',
64	    lastCommandCode: '',
65	    receiveCommandSuccess: false,
66	    runtimeSeconds: 0,
67	    satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
68	    lastErrorReason: '',
69	    globalConfig,
70	    activeCommandConfigs: [],
71	  };
72	
73	  const reader: CommandIngressStateReader = {
74	    getSnapshot() {
75	      return structuredClone(state);
76	    },
77	    globalConfig() {
78	      return state.globalConfig;
79	    },
80	    get loadedSatelliteId() {
81	      return state.loadedSatelliteId;
82	    },
83	    get scoeFramesLoaded() {
84	      return state.scoeFramesLoaded;
85	    },
86	  };
87	
88	  const writer: CommandIngressStateWriter = {
89	    setLoaded(satelliteId, configs) {
90	      state = {
91	        ...state,
92	        loadedSatelliteId: satelliteId,
93	        scoeFramesLoaded: true,
94	        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
95	        activeCommandConfigs: [...configs],
96	      };
97	    },
98	
99	    resetRuntimeState() {
100	      state = {
101	        ...state,
102	        scoeFramesLoaded: false,
103	        loadedSatelliteId: '',
104	        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
105	        commandReceiveCount: 0,
106	        commandSuccessCount: 0,
107	        commandErrorCount: 0,
108	        healthStatus: 'unknown',
109	        linkTestResult: 'unknown',
110	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
111	        lastCommandCode: '',
112	        lastErrorReason: '',
113	        activeCommandConfigs: [],
114	      };
115	    },
116	
117	    updateStatus(patch) {
118	      state = { ...state, ...patch };
119	    },
120	
121	    incrementReceiveCount() {
122	      state = {
123	        ...state,
124	        commandReceiveCount: state.commandReceiveCount + 1,
125	      };
126	    },
127	
128	    incrementSuccessCount() {
129	      state = {
130	        ...state,
131	        commandSuccessCount: state.commandSuccessCount + 1,
132	        receiveCommandSuccess: true,
133	      };
134	    },
135	
136	    incrementErrorCount(reason) {
137	      state = {
138	        ...state,
139	        commandErrorCount: state.commandErrorCount + 1,
140	        lastErrorReason: reason,
141	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
142	      };
143	    },
144	
145	    tickSecond() {
146	      let next = {
147	        ...state,
148	        runtimeSeconds: state.runtimeSeconds + 1,
149	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
150	      };
151	
152	      if (!next.scoeFramesLoaded) {
153	        next = {
154	          ...next,
155	          commandReceiveCount: 0,
156	          commandSuccessCount: 0,
157	          commandErrorCount: 0,
158	          healthStatus: 'unknown',
159	          linkTestResult: 'unknown',
160	        };
161	      } else {
162	        next = {
163	          ...next,
164	          satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: next.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds + 1,
165	        };
166	      }
167	
168	      state = next;
169	    },
170	  };
171	
172	  return { reader, writer };
173	}
174
```

67	    {
68	      frameId: 'frame-001',
69	      instanceId: 'inst-001',
70	      targetId: 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',
71	      fieldMappings: [
72	        { fieldId: 'field-mode', source: 'param', paramId: 'param-mode' },
73	        { fieldId: 'field-fixed', source: 'fixed', fixedValue: 100 },
74	      ],
75	    },
76	  ],
77	  completionConditions: [
78	    {
79	      id: 'cond-001',
80	      label: 'Wait for ack',
81	      sourceFrameId: 'frame-ack',
82	      sourceFieldId: 'field-status',
83	      useParam: false,
84	      targetFixedValue: '1',
85	      operator: 'eq',
86	    },
87	  ],
88	  completionTimeout: 5000,
89	  sendInterval: 100,
90	};
91	
92	export const testCommandConfigs: ScoeCommandConfig[] = [
93	  loadCommandConfig,
94	  unloadCommandConfig,
95	  sendFrameCommandConfig,
96	];
97	
98	// --- State reader fixture ---
99	
100	export function createFakeStateReader(
101	  overrides: Partial<CommandIngressState> = {},
102	): CommandIngressStateReader {
103	  const defaults: CommandIngressState = {
104	    commandReceiveCount: 0,
105	    commandSuccessCount: 0,
106	    commandErrorCount: 0,
107	    loadedSatelliteId: '',
108	    scoeFramesLoaded: false,
109	    healthStatus: 'unknown',
110	    linkTestResult: 'unknown',
111	    lastCommandCode: '',
112	    receiveCommandSuccess: false,
113	    runtimeSeconds: 0,
114	    satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
115	    lastErrorReason: '',
116	    globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,
117	    activeCommandConfigs: [],
118	  };
119	
120	  const state = { ...defaults, ...overrides };
121	  return {
122	    getSnapshot() { return state; },
123	    globalConfig() { return state.globalConfig; },
124	    get loadedSatelliteId() { return state.loadedSatelliteId; },
125	    get scoeFramesLoaded() { return state.scoeFramesLoaded; },
126	  };
127	}
128	
129	// --- Transport event builder ---
130	
131	let nextEventId = 1;
132	
133	export function buildDataEvent(bytes: number[], connectionId = 'conn-tcp-1'): TransportEventSnapshot {
134	  return {
135	    id: `evt-${nextEventId++}`,
136	    kind: 'data',
137	    connectionId,
138	    occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
139	    bytes,
140	    byteLength: bytes.length,
141	  };
142	}
143	
144	// --- SCOE frame byte builder ---
145	
146	export function buildScoeFrame(
147	  commandCode: number,
148	  extraBytes: number[] = [],
149	  config: ScoeGlobalConfig = testGlobalConfig,
150	): number[] {
151	  const fcOffset = config.functionCodeOffset;
152	  const identByte = parseInt(config.scoeIdentifier.replace(/^0x/i, ''), 16);
153	
154	  const frame: number[] = [];
155	  // Ensure we have enough room for the function code area
156	  while (frame.length < fcOffset + 4) {
157	    frame.push(0);
158	  }
159	
160	  frame[fcOffset] = identByte;
161	  frame[fcOffset + 1] = commandCode;
162	  frame[fcOffset + 2] = 0xaa;
163	  frame[fcOffset + 3] = 0xaa;
164	
165	  return [...frame, ...extraBytes];
166	}
167	
168	export function buildScoeFrameWithChecksum(
169	  commandCode: number,
170	  checksumCfg: ChecksumConfig,
171	  extraBytes: number[] = [],
172	  config: ScoeGlobalConfig = testGlobalConfig,
173	): number[] {
174	  const frame = buildScoeFrame(commandCode, extraBytes, config);
175	
176	  // Ensure frame is long enough for checksum area + extra
177	  while (frame.length <= checksumCfg.checksumOffset) {
178	    frame.push(0);
179	  }
180	
181	  // Calculate checksum
182	  let sum = 0;
183	  for (let i = checksumCfg.offset; i < checksumCfg.offset + checksumCfg.length; i++) {
184	    sum += frame[i] ?? 0;
185	  }
186	  frame[checksumCfg.checksumOffset] = sum % 256;
187	
188	  return frame;
189	}
190	
191	export function resetFixtureIds(): void {
192	  nextEventId = 1;
193	}
194

### TaskService Interface
```typescript
export interface TaskService extends TaskReader {
  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
  startTask(instanceId: string): void;
  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void;
  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void;
  stopTask(instanceId: string): void;
  removeTask(instanceId: string): void;
  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>;
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/task-builder.ts",
  "content": "import type {\n  TaskDefinition,\n  TaskStepDefinition,\n  ConditionTerm,\n  FieldVariation,\n} from '@/features/task';\nimport type { ParsedCommand } from './protocol-adapter';\nimport type {\n  ScoeCommandConfig,\n  CompletionConditionConfig,\n} from './types';\n\n/**\n * Build ConditionTerm[] from CompletionConditionConfig[].\n * Supports both fixed-value and parameterized modes (findIndex alignment).\n */\nexport function buildWaitConditions(\n  command: ParsedCommand,\n  condConfigs?: readonly CompletionConditionConfig[],\n): ConditionTerm[] {\n  if (!condConfigs || condConfigs.length === 0) return [];\n\n  return condConfigs.map((condConfig) => {\n    if (condConfig.useParam && condConfig.targetParamId) {\n      const paramValue = command.resolvedParams[condConfig.targetParamId];\n      const param = command.commandConfig.params?.find(\n        (p) => p.id === condConfig.targetParamId,\n      );\n      const optionIndex =\n        param?.options.findIndex((o) => o.value === paramValue) ?? -1;\n      const condOption = condConfig.options?.[optionIndex];\n\n      return {\n        frameId: condConfig.sourceFrameId,\n        fieldId: condConfig.sourceFieldId,\n        operator: condOption?.operator ?? ('eq' as const),\n        threshold: condOption?.matchValue ?? '',\n      };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      frameId: condConfig.sourceFrameId,\n      fieldId: condConfig.sourceFieldId,\n      operator: condConfig.operator ?? ('eq' as const),\n      threshold: condConfig.targetFixedValue ?? '',\n    };\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * SEND_FRAME → TaskDefinition.\n * Uses schedule: { kind: 'immediate' }, one send step per frame mapping,\n * optional delay steps for sendInterval, and a wait-condition step for completion.\n */\nexport function buildSendFrameTask(\n  command: ParsedCommand,\n): TaskDefinition {\n  const config = command.commandConfig;\n  const steps: TaskStepDefinition[] = [];\n\n  for (const mapping of config.frameMappings ?? []) {\n    const userFieldValues: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = {};\n    for (const fm of mapping.fieldMappings) {\n      if (fm.source === 'param' && fm.paramId) {\n        userFieldValues[fm.fieldId] = command.resolvedParams[fm.paramId] ?? '';\n      } else if (fm.fixedValue !== undefined) {\n        userFieldValues[fm.fieldId] = fm.fixedValue;\n      }\n    }\n\n    steps.push({\n      id: `send-${mapping.instanceId}`,\n      kind: 'send',\n      config: {\n        frameId: mapping.frameId,\n        targetId: mapping.targetId,\n        userFieldValues,\n      },\n    });\n\n    if (config.sendInterval && config.sendInterval > 0) {\n      steps.push({\n        id: `delay-after-${mapping.instanceId}`,\n        kind: 'delay',\n        config: { durationMs: config.sendInterval },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const waitConditions = buildWaitConditions(command, config.completionConditions);\n  if (waitConditions.length > 0) {\n    steps.push({\n      id: 'wait-completion',\n      kind: 'wait-condition',\n      config: {\n        conditions: waitConditions,\n        timeoutMs: config.completionTimeout ?? 5000,\n        onTimeout: 'fail',\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  return {\n    id: `scoe-send-${command.commandId}`,\n    name: `SCOE SEND_FRAME: ${config.label}`,\n    schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },\n    steps,\n    errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * READ_FILE_AND_SEND → TaskDefinition.\n * Uses schedule: { kind: 'timer' } + fieldVariations + stopCondition.maxIterations.\n */\nexport function buildReadFileAndSendTask(\n  command: ParsedCommand,\n  fileLines: readonly string[],\n): TaskDefinition {\n  const config = command.commandConfig;\n  const mapping = config.frameMappings?.[0];\n  if (!mapping) throw new Error('No frame mapping for READ_FILE_AND_SEND');\n\n  const fileParam = config.params?.find((p) => p.value?.includes('.txt'));\n  const fileFieldMapping = mapping.fieldMappings.find(\n    (fm) => fm.source === 'param' && fm.paramId === fileParam?.id,\n  );\n\n  const waitConditions = buildWaitConditions(command, config.completionConditions);\n  const steps: TaskStepDefinition[] = [];\n\n  steps.push({\n    id: 'send-rfs',\n    kind: 'send',\n    config: {\n      frameId: mapping.frameId,\n      targetId: mapping.targetId,\n      userFieldValues: {},\n    },\n  });\n\n  if (waitConditions.length > 0) {\n    steps.push({\n      id: 'wait-rfs',\n      kind: 'wait-condition',\n      config: {\n        conditions: waitConditions,\n        timeoutMs: config.completionTimeout ?? 5000,\n        onTimeout: 'fail',\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  const fieldVariations: FieldVariation[] = fileFieldMapping\n    ? [{ fieldId: fileFieldMapping.fieldId, values: [...fileLines] }]\n    : [];\n\n  return {\n    id: `scoe-rfs-${command.commandId}`,\n    name: `SCOE READ_FILE_AND_SEND: ${config.label}`,\n    schedule: { kind: 'timer', intervalMs: config.sendInterval ?? 1000 },\n    steps,\n    fieldVariations: fieldVariations.length > 0 ? fieldVariations : undefined,\n    stopCondition: { maxIterations: fileLines.length },\n    errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },\n  };\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
```
```json
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/handle-load.ts",
  "content": "import type { CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';\nimport type { SatelliteConfig } from '../core/types';\n\n/**\n * G6: satelliteConfigs loaded from config file (in-memory) at assembly time.\n * Passed in via closure when the handler map is created.\n */\nexport function createHandleLoadSatelliteId(\n  satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[],\n): CommandHandler {\n  return async (command, ctx) => {\n    if (ctx.stateReader.loadedSatelliteId !== '') {\n      return { success: false, error: 'Satellite already loaded' };\n    }\n\n    const satOffset = ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().satelliteIdOffset;\n    const rawBytes = command.rawBytes;\n\n    // Extract satellite ID bytes from the command payload\n    const satIdBytes = rawBytes.slice(satOffset, satOffset + 4);\n    const satelliteId = satIdBytes\n      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase())\n      .join('');\n\n    const satConfig = satelliteConfigs.find((s) => s.satelliteId === satelliteId);\n    if (!satConfig) {\n      return { success: false, error: `Unknown satellite ID: ${satelliteId}` };\n    }\n\n    ctx.stateWriter.setLoaded(satelliteId, satConfig.commandConfigs);\n\n    return { success: true };\n  };\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/handle-send-frame.ts",
  "content": "import type { CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';\nimport { buildSendFrameTask } from '../core/task-builder';\nimport { sendAckFrame } from './send-ack-frame';\n\nexport const handleSendFrame: CommandHandler = async (command, ctx) => {\n  if (!ctx.stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {\n    return { success: false, error: 'Satellite not loaded' };\n  }\n\n  const taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(command);\n  const instance = ctx.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n  ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n\n  ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount();\n\n  // Fire-and-forget: monitor task settlement for ack frame\n  ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(() => {\n    const inst = ctx.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {\n      sendAckFrame(command, ctx);\n      ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();\n    } else {\n      ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');\n    }\n  });\n\n  return { success: true, taskId: instance.instanceId };\n};\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/handle-read-file-and-send.ts",
  "content": "import type { CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';\nimport { buildReadFileAndSendTask } from '../core/task-builder';\nimport { sendAckFrame } from './send-ack-frame';\n\nexport const handleReadFileAndSend: CommandHandler = async (command, ctx) => {\n  if (!ctx.stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {\n    return { success: false, error: 'Satellite not loaded' };\n  }\n\n  // Find the file path from command params\n  const fileParam = command.commandConfig.params?.find(\n    (p) => p.value?.includes('.txt'),\n  );\n  if (!fileParam) {\n    return { success: false, error: 'No file parameter found' };\n  }\n\n  const filePath = command.resolvedParams[fileParam.id] ?? fileParam.value;\n  let fileLines: string[];\n  try {\n    fileLines = await ctx.platformFileReader(filePath);\n  } catch (err) {\n    return {\n      success: false,\n      error: `Failed to read file: ${(err as Error).message}`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (fileLines.length === 0) {\n    return { success: false, error: 'File is empty' };\n  }\n\n  const taskDef = buildReadFileAndSendTask(command, fileLines);\n  const instance = ctx.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n  ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n\n  ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount();\n\n  // Fire-and-forget: monitor task settlement for ack frame\n  ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(() => {\n    const inst = ctx.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);\n    if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {\n      sendAckFrame(command, ctx);\n      ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();\n    } else {\n      ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');\n    }\n  });\n\n  return { success: true, taskId: instance.instanceId };\n};\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/send-ack-frame.ts",
  "content": "import type { ParsedCommand } from '../core/protocol-adapter';\nimport type { CommandContext } from '../core/handler';\n\n/**\n * Send acknowledgment frame via sendService after task completes successfully.\n * Uses successFrameId from command config (falling back to global config),\n * resolved through sendService.execute() which handles frame serialization\n * and connection write.\n */\nexport async function sendAckFrame(\n  command: ParsedCommand,\n  ctx: CommandContext,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const successFrameId =\n    command.commandConfig.successFrameId ??\n    ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().successFrameId;\n  if (!successFrameId) return;\n\n  await ctx.sendService.execute({\n    frameId: successFrameId,\n    targetId:\n      command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId ??\n      ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,\n    userFieldValues: {},\n  });\n}\n"
}
```

```
1	import type { TaskService } from '@/features/task';
2	import type { SendService } from '@/features/send';
3	import type { ConnectionService, ConnectionStateSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
4	import type { FrameAssetReader } from '@/features/frame';
5	import type { ParsedCommand } from './protocol-adapter';
6	import type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from './state';
7	import type { ScoeCommandFunction } from './types';
8	import type { SatelliteConfig } from './types';
9	
10	export interface CommandContext {
11	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
12	  readonly sendService: SendService;
13	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
14	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
15	  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;
16	  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;
17	  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;
18	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
19	  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;
20	}
21	
22	export type CommandHandler = (
23	  command: ParsedCommand,
24	  ctx: CommandContext,
25	) => Promise<CommandResult>;
26	
27	export interface CommandResult {
28	  readonly success: boolean;
29	  readonly error?: string;
30	  readonly taskId?: string;
31	}
32	
33	export interface HandlerMapOptions {
34	  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];
35	}
36	
37	export function createHandlerMap(
38	  options: HandlerMapOptions,
39	): ReadonlyMap<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler> {
40	  const {
41	    createHandleLoadSatelliteId,
42	    handleUnloadSatelliteId,
43	    handleHealthCheck,
44	    handleLinkCheck,
45	    handleSendFrame,
46	    handleReadFileAndSend,
47	  } = require('../handlers');
48	
49	  return new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([
50	    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(options.satelliteConfigs)],
51	    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],
52	    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],
53	    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],
54	    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],
55	    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],
56	  ]);
57	}
58	
59	export async function dispatchCommand(
60	  command: ParsedCommand,
61	  handlerMap: ReadonlyMap<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>,
62	  ctx: CommandContext,
63	): Promise<CommandResult> {
64	  const handler = handlerMap.get(command.commandFunction);
65	  if (!handler) {
66	    return { success: false, error: `No handler for: ${command.commandFunction}` };
67	  }
68	  return handler(command, ctx);
69	}
70
```

```json
{

  "content": "import type { TaskService } from '@/features/task';\nimport type { SendService } from '@/features/send';\nimport type { ConnectionService, ConnectionStateSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { FrameAssetReader } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { ConsumerResult } from '@/runtime/consumer-chain';\nimport type { ProtocolAdapter, ParsedCommand } from '../core/protocol-adapter';\nimport type { CommandContext, CommandHandler, CommandResult } from '../core/handler';\nimport type { ScoeCommandFunction, SatelliteConfig } from '../core/types';\nimport type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from '../core/state';\nimport type { ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';\nimport { dispatchCommand } from '../core/handler';\nimport { createScoeProtocolAdapter } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';\nimport { createHandleLoadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-load';\nimport { handleUnloadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-unload';\nimport { handleHealthCheck } from '../handlers/handle-health-check';\nimport { handleLinkCheck } from '../handlers/handle-link-check';\nimport { handleSendFrame } from '../handlers/handle-send-frame';\nimport { handleReadFileAndSend } from '../handlers/handle-read-file-and-send';\nimport { startStatusTimer } from './status-timer';\n\nexport interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {\n  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];\n  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];\n  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];\n  readonly taskService: TaskService;\n  readonly sendService: SendService;\n  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;\n  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;\n  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;\n  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;\n  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;\n  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;\n  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;\n}\n\nexport interface CommandIngressService {\n  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;\n  dispose(): void;\n}\n\nexport function createCommandIngressService(\n  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,\n): CommandIngressService {\n  const {\n    satelliteConfigs,\n    taskService,\n    sendService,\n    frameReader,\n    connectionService,\n    connectionSnapshot,\n    receiveSnapshot,\n    platformFileReader,\n    stateReader,\n    stateWriter,\n  } = options;\n\n  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds\n  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();\n\n  // Handler map\n  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([\n    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],\n    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],\n    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],\n    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],\n    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],\n    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],\n  ]);\n\n  const ctx: CommandContext = {\n    taskService,\n    sendService,\n    frameReader,\n    connectionService,\n    connectionSnapshot,\n    receiveSnapshot,\n    platformFileReader,\n    stateReader,\n    stateWriter,\n  };\n\n  // Create protocol adapter with dispatch wired in\n  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,\n    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,\n    stateReader,\n  });\n\n  // Override consume to dispatch parsed commands\n  const originalConsume = adapter.consume.bind(adapter);\n  const dispatchingConsumer = {\n    ...adapter,\n    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {\n      const result = await originalConsume(events);\n\n      // Check for disconnected connections and clean up\n      cleanupDisconnectedConnections();\n\n      return result;\n    },\n  };\n\n  // Start status timer\n  const stopTimer = startStatusTimer(stateWriter);\n\n  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {\n    const snapshot = connectionSnapshot();\n    // connectionSnapshot returns ConnectionStateSnapshot which may have active connections\n    // For now, rely on the tracking map being cleaned up when tasks settle\n    // Full disconnect detection will be integrated when connection events are available\n    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {\n      // Check if connection is still active through connection facts\n      const facts = connectionService.listConnectionFacts?.();\n      const isActive = facts?.some((f) => f.connectionId === connId);\n      if (!isActive) {\n        for (const taskId of taskIds) {\n          try {\n            taskService.stopTask(taskId);\n          } catch {\n            // Task may already be settled\n          }\n        }\n        trackingMap.delete(connId);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  function trackTask(connectionId: string, taskId: string): void {\n    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);\n    if (!set) {\n      set = new Set();\n      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);\n    }\n    set.add(taskId);\n\n    // Auto-cleanup when task settles\n    taskService.onSettled(taskId).then(() => {\n      set!.delete(taskId);\n      if (set!.size === 0) {\n        trackingMap.delete(connectionId);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  function dispose(): void {\n    stopTimer();\n    // Stop all tracked tasks\n    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {\n      for (const taskId of taskIds) {\n        try {\n          taskService.stopTask(taskId);\n        } catch {\n          // Ignore errors during cleanup\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    trackingMap.clear();\n  }\n\n  return {\n    adapter: dispatchingConsumer,\n    dispose,\n  };\n}\n"
}
```
