1	import type { TaskService } from '@/features/task';
2	import type { SendService } from '@/features/send';
3	import type { ConnectionService, ConnectionStateSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
4	import type { FrameAssetReader } from '@/features/frame';
5	import type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
6	import type { ConsumerResult } from '@/runtime/consumer-chain';
7	import type { ProtocolAdapter, ParsedCommand } from '../core/protocol-adapter';
8	import type { CommandContext, CommandHandler, CommandResult } from '../core/handler';
9	import type { ScoeCommandFunction, SatelliteConfig } from '../core/types';
10	import type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from '../core/state';
11	import type { ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';
12	import { dispatchCommand } from '../core/handler';
13	import { createScoeProtocolAdapter } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';
14	import { createHandleLoadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-load';
15	import { handleUnloadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-unload';
16	import { handleHealthCheck } from '../handlers/handle-health-check';
17	import { handleLinkCheck } from '../handlers/handle-link-check';
18	import { handleSendFrame } from '../handlers/handle-send-frame';
19	import { handleReadFileAndSend } from '../handlers/handle-read-file-and-send';
20	import { startStatusTimer } from './status-timer';
21	
22	export interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {
23	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];
24	  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];
25	  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];
26	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
27	  readonly sendService: SendService;
28	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
29	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
30	  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;
31	  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;
32	  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;
33	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
34	  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;
35	}
36	
37	export interface CommandIngressService {
38	  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;
39	  dispose(): void;
40	}
41	
42	export function createCommandIngressService(
43	  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,
44	): CommandIngressService {
45	  const {
46	    satelliteConfigs,
47	    taskService,
48	    sendService,
49	    frameReader,
50	    connectionService,
51	    connectionSnapshot,
52	    receiveSnapshot,
53	    platformFileReader,
54	    stateReader,
55	    stateWriter,
56	  } = options;
57	
58	  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds
59	  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
60	
61	  // Handler map
62	  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([
63	    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],
64	    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],
65	    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],
66	    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],
67	    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],
68	    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],
69	  ]);
70	
71	  const ctx: CommandContext = {
72	    taskService,
73	    sendService,
74	    frameReader,
75	    connectionService,
76	    connectionSnapshot,
77	    receiveSnapshot,
78	    platformFileReader,
79	    stateReader,
80	    stateWriter,
81	  };
82	
83	  // Create protocol adapter with dispatch wired in
84	  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({
85	    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,
86	    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,
87	    stateReader,
88	  });
89	
90	  // Override consume to dispatch parsed commands
91	  const originalConsume = adapter.consume.bind(adapter);
92	  const dispatchingConsumer = {
93	    ...adapter,
94	    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {
95	      const result = await originalConsume(events);
96	
97	      // Check for disconnected connections and clean up
98	      cleanupDisconnectedConnections();
99	
100	      return result;
101	    },
102	  };
103	
104	  // Start status timer
105	  const stopTimer = startStatusTimer(stateWriter);
106	
107	  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {
108	    const snapshot = connectionSnapshot();
109	    // connectionSnapshot returns ConnectionStateSnapshot which may have active connections
110	    // For now, rely on the tracking map being cleaned up when tasks settle
111	    // Full disconnect detection will be integrated when connection events are available
112	    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
113	      // Check if connection is still active through connection facts
114	      const facts = connectionService.listConnectionFacts?.();
115	      const isActive = facts?.some((f) => f.connectionId === connId);
116	      if (!isActive) {
117	        for (const taskId of taskIds) {
118	          try {
119	            taskService.stopTask(taskId);
120	          } catch {
121	            // Task may already be settled
122	          }
123	        }
124	        trackingMap.delete(connId);
125	      }
126	    }
127	  }
128	
129	  function trackTask(connectionId: string, taskId: string): void {
130	    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
131	    if (!set) {
132	      set = new Set();
133	      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);
134	    }
135	    set.add(taskId);
136	
137	    // Auto-cleanup when task settles
138	    taskService.onSettled(taskId).then(() => {
139	      set!.delete(taskId);
140	      if (set!.size === 0) {
141	        trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
142	      }
143	    });
144	  }
145	
146	  function dispose(): void {
147	    stopTimer();
148	    // Stop all tracked tasks
149	    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
150	      for (const taskId of taskIds) {
151	        try {
152	          taskService.stopTask(taskId);
153	        } catch {
154	          // Ignore errors during cleanup
155	        }
156	      }
157	    }
158	    trackingMap.clear();
159	  }
160	
161	  return {
162	    adapter: dispatchingConsumer,
163	    dispose,
164	  };
165	}
166

'../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';\nimport { createHandleLoadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-load';\nimport { handleUnloadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-unload';\nimport { handleHealthCheck } from '../handlers/handle-health-check';\nimport { handleLinkCheck } from '../handlers/handle-link-check';\nimport { handleSendFrame } from '../handlers/handle-send-frame';\nimport { handleReadFileAndSend } from '../handlers/handle-read-file-and-send';\nimport { startStatusTimer } from './status-timer';\n\nexport interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {\n  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];\n  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];\n  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];\n  readonly taskService: TaskService;\n  readonly sendService: SendService;\n  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;\n  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;\n  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;\n  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;\n  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;\n  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;\n  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;\n}\n\nexport interface CommandIngressService {\n  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;\n  dispose(): void;\n}\n\nexport function createCommandIngressService(\n  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,\n): CommandIngressService {\n  const {\n    satelliteConfigs,\n    taskService,\n    sendService,\n    frameReader,\n    connectionService,\n    connectionSnapshot,\n    receiveSnapshot,\n    platformFileReader,\n    stateReader,\n    stateWriter,\n  } = options;\n\n  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds\n  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();\n\n  // Handler map\n  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([\n    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],\n    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],\n    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],\n    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],\n    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],\n    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],\n  ]);\n\n  const ctx: CommandContext = {\n    taskService,\n    sendService,\n    frameReader,\n    connectionService,\n    connectionSnapshot,\n    receiveSnapshot,\n    platformFileReader,\n    stateReader,\n    stateWriter,\n  };\n\n  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands to handlers\n  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {\n    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);\n    if (result.taskId) {\n      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId);\n    }\n    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();\n  }\n\n  // Create protocol adapter with onCommand dispatch\n  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,\n    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,\n    stateReader,\n    onCommand,\n  });\n\n  // Start status timer\n  const stopTimer = startStatusTimer(stateWriter);\n\n  function trackTask(connectionId: string, taskId: string): void {\n    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);\n    if (!set) {\n      set = new Set();\n      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);\n    }\n    set.add(taskId);\n\n    taskService.onSettled(taskId).then(() => {\n      set!.delete(taskId);\n      if (set!.size === 0) {\n        trackingMap.delete(connectionId);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {\n    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {\n      const fact = connectionService.getConnectionFact(connId);\n      if (!fact || fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected') {\n        for (const taskId of taskIds) {\n          try {\n            taskService.stopTask(taskId);\n          } catch {\n            // Task may already be settled\n          }\n        }\n        trackingMap.delete(connId);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  function dispose(): void {\n    stopTimer();\n    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {\n      for (const taskId of taskIds) {\n        try {\n          taskService.stopTask(taskId);\n        } catch {\n          // Ignore errors during cleanup\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    trackingMap.clear();\n  }\n\n  return {\n    adapter,\n    dispose,\n  };\n}\n"
}

268	  it('phase 1: only accepts LOAD command when scoeFramesLoaded=false', async () => {
269	    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: false });

271	      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,
272	      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,
273	      stateReader,
274	    });
275	
276	    const loadEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x01)); // LOAD
277	    const sendEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x05)); // SEND_FRAME
278	    const unloadEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x02)); // UNLOAD
279	
280	    const result = await adapter.consume([loadEvent, sendEvent, unloadEvent]);
281	    expect(result.consumed).toEqual([loadEvent]);
282	    expect(result.remaining).toEqual([sendEvent, unloadEvent]);
283	  });
284	
285	  it('phase 2: accepts all commands when scoeFramesLoaded=true', async () => {

293	    const loadEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x01));
294	    const sendEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x05));

296	
297	    const result = await adapter.consume([loadEvent, sendEvent, unloadEvent]);
298	    expect(result.consumed).toEqual([loadEvent, sendEvent, unloadEvent]);
299	    expect(result.remaining).toEqual([]);
300	  });
301	
302	  it('passes non-SCOE events through as remaining', async () => {
303	    const stateReader = createFakeStateReader({ scoeFramesLoaded: true });
304	    const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({
305	      globalConfig: testGlobalConfig,
306	      commandConfigs: testCommandConfigs,
307	      stateReader,
308	    });
309	
310	    const scoeEvent = buildDataEvent(buildScoeFrame(0x01));
311	    const normalEvent = buildDataEvent([0xDE, 0xAD, 0xBE, 0xEF, 0x00, 0x00]);
312	    const normalEvent2 = buildDataEvent([0x10, 0x20, 0x30, 0x40, 0x50, 0x60]);
313	
314	    const result = await adapter.consume([scoeEvent, normalEvent, normalEvent2]);
315	    expect(result.consumed).toEqual([scoeEvent]);
316	    expect(result.remaining).toEqual([normalEvent, normalEvent2]);
317	  });
318	});

321	
322	describe('CommandIngressState container', () => {
323	  it('starts with correct initial values', async () => {
324	    const { createCommandIngressState } = await import('../core/state');
325	    const { reader } = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);
326	
327	    expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(false);
328	    expect(reader.loadedSatelliteId).toBe('');
329	    const snap = reader.getSnapshot();

339	    writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', testCommandConfigs);

342	    expect(reader.getSnapshot().activeCommandConfigs).toEqual(testCommandConfigs);
343	  });

345	  it('resetRuntimeState clears all runtime data', async () => {

348	
349	    writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', testCommandConfigs);

352	
353	    writer.resetRuntimeState();

364	    writer.incrementSuccessCount();

 return {\n    commandId: 'scoe-cmd-test-1',\n    commandCode: '05',\n    commandFunction: 'send_frame',\n    rawBytes: [0x01, 0x05, 0xaa, 0xaa],\n    resolvedParams: { 'param-mode': 'mode_a' },\n    commandConfig: sendFrameCommandConfig,\n    connectionId: 'conn-tcp-1',\n    occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    ...overrides,\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe('buildWaitConditions', () => {\n  it('returns empty array when no conditions', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    expect(buildWaitConditions(cmd, undefined)).toEqual([]);\n    expect(buildWaitConditions(cmd, [])).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('builds fixed-value condition term', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    const conditions = buildWaitConditions(cmd, sendFrameCommandConfig.completionConditions);\n\n    expect(conditions).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(conditions[0]).toEqual({\n      frameId: 'frame-ack',\n      fieldId: 'field-status',\n      operator: 'eq',\n      threshold: '1',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('builds parameterized condition with option index alignment', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      resolvedParams: { 'param-mode': 'mode_b' },\n      commandConfig: {\n        ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n        params: [\n          {\n            id: 'param-mode',\n            label: 'Mode',\n            value: '',\n            type: 'string',\n            offset: 13,\n            length: 1,\n            options: [\n              { label: 'Mode A', value: 'mode_a', receiveCode: '01' },\n              { label: 'Mode B', value: 'mode_b', receiveCode: '02' },\n            ],\n          },\n        ],\n        completionConditions: [\n          {\n            id: 'cond-param',\n            label: 'Wait for param',\n            sourceFrameId: 'frame-ack',\n            sourceFieldId: 'field-result',\n            useParam: true,\n            targetParamId: 'param-mode',\n            options: [\n              { operator: 'eq' as const, matchValue: '0A' },\n              { operator: 'gte' as const, matchValue: '0B' },\n            ],\n          },\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n\n    const conditions = buildWaitConditions(cmd, cmd.commandConfig.completionConditions);\n    expect(conditions).toHaveLength(1);\n    // mode_b is at index 1 → options[1] = { operator: 'gte', matchValue: '0B' }\n    expect(conditions[0]).toEqual({\n      frameId: 'frame-ack',\n      fieldId: 'field-result',\n      operator: 'gte',\n      threshold: '0B',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('falls back to eq when param option index not found', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      resolvedParams: { 'param-mode': 'unknown_value' },\n      commandConfig: {\n        ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n        params: [\n          {\n            id: 'param-mode',\n            label: 'Mode',\n            value: '',\n            type: 'string',\n            offset: 13,\n            length: 1,\n            options: [\n              { label: 'Mode A', value: 'mode_a', receiveCode: '01' },\n            ],\n          },\n        ],\n        completionConditions: [\n          {\n            id: 'cond-param',\n            label: 'Wait for param',\n            sourceFrameId: 'frame-ack',\n            sourceFieldId: 'field-result',\n            useParam: true,\n            targetParamId: 'param-mode',\n          },\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n\n    const conditions = buildWaitConditions(cmd, cmd.commandConfig.completionConditions);\n    expect(conditions[0]?.operator).toBe('eq');\n    expect(conditions[0]?.threshold).toBe('');\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('buildSendFrameTask', () => {\n  it('builds TaskDefinition with immediate schedule', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    const taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(cmd);\n\n    expect(taskDef.schedule).toEqual({ kind: 'immediate' });\n    expect(taskDef.errorPolicy).toEqual({ onFailure: 'stop' });\n    expect(taskDef.id).toMatch(/^scoe-send-/);\n    expect(taskDef.name).toContain('SEND_FRAME');\n  });\n\n  it('creates send step with resolved param field values', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    const taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(cmd);\n\n    const sendStep = taskDef.steps.find((s) => s.kind === 'send');\n    expect(sendStep).toBeDefined();\n    expect(sendStep!.kind).toBe('send');\n    if (sendStep!.kind === 'send') {\n      expect(sendStep.config.userFieldValues).toEqual({\n        'field-mode': 'mode_a',\n        'field-fixed': 100,\n      });\n      expect(sendStep.config.frameId).toBe('frame-001');\n      expect(sendStep.config.targetId).toBe('network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote');\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('creates delay step when sendInterval > 0', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    const taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(cmd);\n\n    const delayStep = taskDef.steps.find((s) => s.kind === 'delay');\n    expect(delayStep).toBeDefined();\n    if (delayStep!.kind === 'delay') {\n      expect(delayStep.config.durationMs).toBe(100);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('creates wait-condition step when completion conditions exist', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    const taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(cmd);\n\n    const waitStep = taskDef.steps.find((s) => s.kind === 'wait-condition');\n    expect(waitStep).toBeDefined();\n    if (waitStep!.kind === 'wait-condition') {\n      expect(waitStep.config.timeoutMs).toBe(5000);\n      expect(waitStep.config.onTimeout).toBe('fail');\n      expect(waitStep.config.conditions).toHaveLength(1);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('skips delay step when sendInterval is 0 or undefined', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandConfig: {\n        ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n        sendInterval: undefined,\n      },\n    });\n    const taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(cmd);\n\n    expect(taskDef.steps.find((s) => s.kind === 'delay')).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('buildReadFileAndSendTask', () => {\n  it('throws when no frame mappings', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandConfig: {\n        ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n        frameMappings: undefined,\n      },\n    });\n    expect(() => buildReadFileAndSendTask(cmd, ['line1'])).toThrow('No frame mapping');\n  });\n\n  it('builds TaskDefinition with timer schedule and fieldVariations', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandConfig: {\n        ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n        params: [\n          {\n            id: 'param-file',\n            label: 'File',\n            value: 'test.txt',\n            type: 'string',\n            offset: 13,\n            length: 1,\n            options: [],\n          },\n        ],\n        frameMappings: [\n          {\n            frameId: 'frame-001',\n            instanceId: 'inst-001',\n            targetId: 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',\n            fieldMappings: [\n              { fieldId: 'field-data', source: 'param', paramId: 'param-file' },\n            ],\n          },\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n\n    const fileLines = ['data1', 'data2', 'data3'];\n    const taskDef = buildReadFileAndSendTask(cmd, fileLines);\n\n    expect(taskDef.schedule).toEqual({ kind: 'timer', intervalMs: 100 });\n    expect(taskDef.stopCondition).toEqual({ maxIterations: 3 });\n    expect(taskDef.fieldVariations).toEqual([\n      { fieldId: 'field-data', values: ['data1', 'data2', 'data3'] },\n    ]);\n    expect(taskDef.id).toMatch(/^scoe-rfs-/);\n    expect(taskDef.name).toContain('READ_FILE_AND_SEND');\n  });\n\n  it('uses default intervalMs when sendInterval is undefined', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandConfig: {\n        ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n        sendInterval: undefined,\n        frameMappings: [\n          {\n            frameId: 'frame-001',\n            instanceId: 'inst-001',\n            targetId: 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',\n            fieldMappings: [],\n          },\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n\n    const taskDef = buildReadFileAndSendTask(cmd, ['line1']);\n    expect(taskDef.schedule).toEqual({ kind: 'timer', intervalMs: 1000 });\n  });\n\n  it('omits fieldVariations when no file param mapping found', () => {\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandConfig: {\n        ...sendFrameCommandConfig,\n        params: [],\n        frameMappings: [\n          {\n            frameId: 'frame-001',\n            instanceId: 'inst-001',\n            targetId: 'network:scoe-udp:scoe-udp-remote',\n            fieldMappings: [],\n          },\n        ],\n      },\n    });\n\n    const taskDef = buildReadFileAndSendTask(cmd, ['line1']);\n    expect(taskDef.fieldVariations).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n"

  "content": "import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\nimport { dispatchCommand } from '../core/handler';\nimport type { CommandHandler, CommandContext, CommandResult } from '../core/handler';\nimport type { ParsedCommand } from '../core/protocol-adapter';\nimport { createHandleLoadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-load';\nimport { handleUnloadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-unload';\nimport { handleHealthCheck } from '../handlers/handle-health-check';\nimport { handleLinkCheck } from '../handlers/handle-link-check';\nimport { handleSendFrame } from '../handlers/handle-send-frame';\nimport { handleReadFileAndSend } from '../handlers/handle-read-file-and-send';\nimport {\n  createFakeStateReader,\n  testGlobalConfig,\n  sendFrameCommandConfig,\n  loadCommandConfig,\n} from '../fixtures/command-ingress-fixtures';\nimport type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from '../core/state';\nimport { createCommandIngressState } from '../core/state';\n\nfunction createFakeContext(overrides: Partial<CommandContext> = {}): CommandContext {\n  const stateContainer = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n  const taskService = {\n    createTask: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ instanceId: 'task-1' }),\n    startTask: vi.fn(),\n    stopTask: vi.fn(),\n    onSettled: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),\n    getInstance: vi.fn().mockReturnValue(undefined),\n  };\n\n  return {\n    taskService: taskService as any,\n    sendService: {\n      execute: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ success: true }),\n    } as any,\n    frameReader: { getFrame: vi.fn().mockReturnValue(undefined) } as any,\n    connectionService: {} as any,\n    connectionSnapshot: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({}),\n    receiveSnapshot: vi.fn().mockReturnValue({}),\n    platformFileReader: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(['line1', 'line2']),\n    stateReader: stateContainer.reader,\n    stateWriter: stateContainer.writer,\n    ...overrides,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction buildParsedCommand(overrides: Partial<ParsedCommand> = {}): ParsedCommand {\n  return {\n    commandId: 'scoe-cmd-test-1',\n    commandCode: '05',\n    commandFunction: 'send_frame',\n    rawBytes: [0x01, 0x05, 0xaa, 0xaa],\n    resolvedParams: {},\n    commandConfig: sendFrameCommandConfig,\n    connectionId: 'conn-tcp-1',\n    occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    ...overrides,\n  };\n}\n\n// --- dispatchCommand ---\n\ndescribe('dispatchCommand', () => {\n  it('returns error when no handler registered', async () => {\n    const handlerMap = new Map<string, CommandHandler>();\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    const ctx = createFakeContext();\n\n    const result = await dispatchCommand(cmd, handlerMap, ctx);\n    expect(result.success).toBe(false);\n    expect(result.error).toContain('No handler');\n  });\n\n  it('dispatches to registered handler', async () => {\n    const mockHandler = vi.fn<Promise<CommandResult>, [ParsedCommand, CommandContext]>()\n      .mockResolvedValue({ success: true });\n    const handlerMap = new Map([['send_frame', mockHandler]]);\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand();\n    const ctx = createFakeContext();\n\n    const result = await dispatchCommand(cmd, handlerMap, ctx);\n    expect(result.success).toBe(true);\n    expect(mockHandler).toHaveBeenCalledWith(cmd, ctx);\n  });\n});\n\n// --- handleLoadSatelliteId ---\n\ndescribe('handleLoadSatelliteId', () => {\n  const satelliteConfigs = [\n    {\n      satelliteId: '00000001',\n      messageIdentifier: '01',\n      sourceIdentifier: '02',\n      destinationIdentifier: '03',\n      modelId: '04',\n      commandConfigs: [sendFrameCommandConfig],\n    },\n  ];\n\n  it('rejects when satellite already loaded', async () => {\n    const stateContainer = createCommandIngressState(testGlobalConfig);\n    stateContainer.writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', []);\n    const ctx = createFakeContext({\n      stateReader: stateContainer.reader,\n      stateWriter: stateContainer.writer,\n    });\n\n    const handler = createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs);\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandFunction: 'load_satellite_id',\n      commandCode: '01',\n      commandConfig: loadCommandConfig,\n      rawBytes: new Array(20).fill(0),\n    });\n    // Put satellite ID bytes at offset 8 (4 bytes: 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01)\n    cmd.rawBytes[8] = 0x00;\n    cmd.rawBytes[9] = 0x00;\n    cmd.rawBytes[10] = 0x00;\n    cmd.rawBytes[11] = 0x01;\n\n    const result = await handler(cmd, ctx);\n    expect(result.success).toBe(false);\n    expect(result.error).toContain('already loaded');\n  });\n\n  it('loads satellite when ID matches config', async () => {\n    const ctx = createFakeContext();\n    const handler = createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs);\n    const rawBytes = new Array(20).fill(0);\n    rawBytes[8] = 0x00;\n    rawBytes[9] = 0x00;\n    rawBytes[10] = 0x00;\n    rawBytes[11] = 0x01;\n\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandFunction: 'load_satellite_id',\n      commandCode: '01',\n      commandConfig: loadCommandConfig,\n      rawBytes,\n    });\n\n    const result = await handler(cmd, ctx);\n    expect(result.success).toBe(true);\n    expect(ctx.stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('rejects unknown satellite ID', async () => {\n    const ctx = createFakeContext();\n    const handler = createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs);\n    const rawBytes = new Array(20).fill(0xff);\n\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({\n      commandFunction: 'load_satellite_id',\n      commandCode: '01',\n      commandConfig: loadCommandConfig,\n      rawBytes,\n    });\n\n    const result = await handler(cmd, ctx);\n    expect(result.success).toBe(false);\n    expect(result.error).toContain('Unknown satellite');\n  });\n});\n\n// --- handleUnloadSatelliteId ---\n\ndescribe('handleUnloadSatelliteId', () => {\n  it('rejects when no satellite loaded', async () => {\n    const ctx = createFakeContext();\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({ commandFunction: 'unload_satellite_id' });\n\n    const result = await handleUnloadSatelliteId(cmd, ctx);\n    expect(result.success).toBe(false);\n    expect(result.error).toContain('No satellite');\n  });\n\n  it('resets runtime state when loaded', async () => {\n    const ctx = createFakeContext();\n    ctx.stateWriter.setLoaded('SAT-01', []);\n    ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount();\n\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({ commandFunction: 'unload_satellite_id' });\n    const result = await handleUnloadSatelliteId(cmd, ctx);\n\n    expect(result.success).toBe(true);\n    expect(ctx.stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(false);\n    expect(ctx.stateReader.loadedSatelliteId).toBe('');\n  });\n});\n\n// --- handleHealthCheck / handleLinkCheck ---\n\ndescribe('handleHealthCheck', () => {\n  it('always succeeds and reads connection snapshot', async () => {\n    const ctx = createFakeContext();\n    const cmd = buildParsedCommand({ commandFunction: 'health_check' });\n\n    const result = await handleHealthCheck(cmd, ctx);\n    expect(result.success).toBe(true);\n    expect(ctx.connectionSnapshot).toHaveBeenCalled();\n ```
I now have all the evidence needed to produce the full review. Let me compile the findings.

---

**VERDICT: ACCEPT-WITH-RESERVATIONS**

**Overall Assessment**: The implementation faithfully follows the design document across all major sections. The six handlers, task builders, state machine, protocol adapter, and service assembly are all present and structurally correct. I found one type mismatch between `buildWaitConditions` and the actual `ConditionTerm` type, one minor deviation in `buildReadFileAndSendTask` vs the design's `fieldVariations` handling, and several minor issues. No critical blockers.

**Pre-commitment Predictions vs Actual**:
1. *Precondition logic edge cases* -- Predicted issues with null checks. Actual: preconditions are correctly implemented across all 6 handlers. No issues found.
2. *`buildSendFrameTask` schedule structure* -- Predicted possible mismatch. Actual: `{ kind: 'immediate' }` matches design exactly.
3. *Wait-condition findIndex alignment* -- Predicted subtle issues. Actual: logic is correct and matches design.
4. *Connection disconnect cleanup lifecycle check* -- Predicted deviation from design. Actual: implementation is simplified but functionally sound (see Finding 5).

---

**Critical Findings** (blocks execution): None.

**Major Findings** (causes significant rework):

**1. [SS5.3] `buildWaitConditions` always returns `threshold: string`, but `ConditionTerm.threshold` is `string | number`**

Design SS5.3 and implementation at `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/task-builder.ts:37` and line 45:

```typescript
// line 37 (parameterized mode)
threshold: condOption?.matchValue ?? '',
// line 45 (fixed-value mode)
threshold: condConfig.targetFixedValue ?? '',
```

The `ConditionTerm` type in `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/core/types.ts:30` declares:
```typescript
readonly threshold: string | number;
```

Both branches always produce a `string` (empty string fallback `''`). This is not a TypeScript error (string is assignable to `string | number`), so compilation passes. However, if a completion condition configuration ever needs a numeric threshold (e.g., `gt 100`), the current code would pass it as the string `'100'`, and the condition evaluator on the task side would need to handle string-to-number coercion. The design itself also uses `string` for `matchValue` and `targetFixedValue`, so this is actually consistent with the design's data model.

- Confidence: MEDIUM (this is consistent with the design's type choices, but the design may have an implicit assumption gap)
- Why this matters: If task-real's condition evaluator does strict type comparison (`===`), numeric thresholds passed as strings would never match.
- Fix: Either (a) confirm that the task condition evaluator handles string thresholds for numeric comparisons, or (b) add a `Number()` conversion when the condition's field is known to be numeric. This is a design-level question, not an implementation deviation per se.

**Self-audit**: Downgrading from MAJOR to a note. The design explicitly types `matchValue` and `targetFixedValue` as `string`, so the implementation correctly follows the design. If there is a mismatch, it is in the design, not the implementation. Moving to Open Questions.

**2. [SS7.2] `sendAckFrame` does not call `frameReader.getFrame()` before sending -- design includes a guard check**

Design SS7.2 specifies:
```typescript
const frame = ctx.frameReader.getFrame(successFrameId);
if (!frame) return;
```

Implementation at `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/send-ack-frame.ts:19-25`:
```typescript
await ctx.sendService.execute({
  frameId: successFrameId,
  targetId:
    command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId ??
    ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,
  userFieldValues: {},
});
```

The implementation skips the `frameReader.getFrame()` guard and delegates directly to `sendService.execute()`. If `sendService.execute()` internally validates the frameId exists (by looking up the frame definition), then the guard is redundant and the implementation is fine. If it does not, a misconfigured `successFrameId` would cause an error at send time rather than being silently skipped.

- Confidence: HIGH
- Why this matters: The design explicitly adds this guard as a safety check. Its omission means errors that could be silently handled will propagate as exceptions.
- Fix: Add `const frame = ctx.frameReader.getFrame(successFrameId); if (!frame) return;` before the `sendService.execute()` call, matching the design.

**Self-audit**: LOW confidence that this is a real problem. `sendService.execute()` likely validates frame existence internally. Moving to Open Questions.

---

**Minor Findings**:

**3. [SS5.1] `buildSendFrameTask` does not receive `ctx` parameter -- design signature includes it**

Design SS5.1: `function buildSendFrameTask(command: ParsedCommand, ctx: CommandContext): TaskDefinition`
Implementation at `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/core/task-builder.ts:55-56`:
```typescript
export function buildSendFrameTask(
  command: ParsedCommand,
): TaskDefinition {
```

The `ctx` parameter is not needed in the current implementation (all data comes from `command.commandConfig`), so this is a harmless simplification. The signature deviation is cosmetic.

**4. [SS8.3] `status-timer.ts` does not return a cleanup function matching the design pattern**

Design SS8.3:
```typescript
return setInterval(() => { stateWriter.tickSecond(); }, intervalMs);
```
(implying the raw `setInterval` handle is returned directly)

Implementation at `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/services/status-timer.ts:7-11`:
```typescript
const handle = setInterval(() => {
  stateWriter.tickSecond();
}, intervalMs);
return () => clearInterval(handle);
```

The implementation wraps the handle in a `() => clearInterval(handle)` closure. This is actually **better** than the design (the design's pseudocode would return a `NodeJS.Timeout` number, requiring the caller to know to call `clearInterval`). Not a deviation in the wrong direction.

**5. [SS12] Connection disconnect cleanup: implementation checks `fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected'` but also checks `!fact` (deleted connection)**

Design SS12.2 specifies checking connection state to detect disconnection. Implementation at `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/services/command-ingress-service.ts:117-131`:
```typescript
function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {
  for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
    const fact = connectionService.getConnectionFact(connId);
    if (!fact || fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected') {
      for (const taskId of taskIds) {
        try { taskService.stopTask(taskId); } catch { /* ... */ }
      }
      trackingMap.delete(connId);
    }
  }
}
```

This is more thorough than the design -- it also handles `!fact` (connection entirely removed from the system), which the design does not explicitly mention. This is a positive deviation. However, the cleanup is only called during `onCommand`, meaning if no new SCOE commands arrive after a disconnect, running tasks for that connection are never cleaned up until the next command or `dispose()`. The design says "在 routingTick 的消费者链流程中检测" (detect during routingTick consumer chain), and `cleanupDisconnectedConnections()` IS called from `onCommand` which is invoked during the consumer chain's `consume()` flow, so this matches the design intent.

**6. [SS4.3.1] LOAD handler extracts satellite ID with hardcoded length of 4 bytes**

Design: "卫星 ID 从 `command.rawBytes[satelliteIdOffset..]` 提取"
Implementation at `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/handle-load.ts:20`:
```typescript
const satIdBytes = rawBytes.slice(satOffset, satOffset + 4);
```

The design does not specify the byte length. The implementation hardcodes 4 bytes. If different satellites have different ID lengths, this would be incorrect. However, the old system likely uses a fixed 4-byte satellite ID, so this is probably correct in practice.

**7. [SS4.3.5/6] Handlers register `onSettled` but `onSettled` resolves immediately for already-settled tasks**

Looking at `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/services/task-service.ts:229-231`:
```typescript
async onSettled(instanceId) {
  const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
  if (!inst || isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
```

If `onSettled` is called after a task has already settled, it returns immediately without resolving the promise. This means the `.then()` callback in the handlers would never fire for a task that settles before `onSettled` is registered. However, since `startTask` is called immediately before `onSettled` registration, and the execution loop is async (`runExecutionLoop` is fire-and-forget), the registration should happen before the task can settle. This is a timing concern but not a deviation from the design.

---

**What's Missing** (gaps, unhandled edge cases, unstated assumptions):

- The design specifies `runtimeSeconds` in the state (SS8.1) but the `tickSecond` in the implementation increments `runtimeSeconds` unconditionally (line 148), which matches the design. However, `runtimeSeconds` is never reset by `resetRuntimeState()` -- it keeps ticking across LOAD/UNLOAD cycles. This matches the design spec for `resetRuntimeState()` which does not list `runtimeSeconds` as a reset field, but it means `runtimeSeconds` is a monotonically increasing counter since service creation, not since the last satellite load. This may or may not be the intended behavior.

- The design (SS4.3.5 step 7) specifies `ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount()` is called in the handler. The implementation calls it at line 14 of `handle-send-frame.ts` and line 37 of `handle-read-file-and-send.ts`. However, the design also implies `incrementReceiveCount` should be called for ALL command handlers (the design section SS8.3 references `commandReceiveCount` as a global stat). Looking at the design more carefully, SS4.3.5 and SS4.3.6 specifically list `incrementReceiveCount()` as a step, while the other 4 handlers do NOT. The implementation matches this exactly -- only SEND_FRAME and READ_FILE_AND_SEND call `incrementReceiveCount()`. This is correct per the design.

- The `handle-load.ts` implementation does not call `ctx.stateWriter.updateStatus(...)` to set `scoeFramesLoaded: true, loadedSatelliteId, satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0` as separate step 5 in the design. Instead, `ctx.stateWriter.setLoaded(...)` handles all of this internally (state.ts:89-97 sets `loadedSatelliteId`, `scoeFramesLoaded: true`, `satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0`). This is a correct coalescing of the design's steps 4-5 into a single writer call. No issue.

---

**Ambiguity Risks**: None identified. The design is detailed enough that each step has a single clear interpretation.

---

**Multi-Perspective Notes**:

- **Executor**: The implementation can be followed step-by-step from the design. The `buildReadFileAndSendTask` file parameter discovery logic (finding `.txt` in `param.value`) is a pragmatic heuristic that works but is fragile -- if a file parameter does not contain `.txt`, it would fail silently. However, this matches the design exactly.

- **Stakeholder**: All 6 handlers are implemented. The two-phase state machine (scoeFramesLoaded gating) is correctly implemented in the protocol adapter. The tracking map for connection cleanup is present. The feature delivers what the design promises.

- **Skeptic**: The strongest argument against this implementation is the `threshold: string` vs `string | number` question in `ConditionTerm`. If task-real's condition evaluator does strict type comparison on numeric fields, string thresholds would never match. But this is a design-level issue, not an implementation deviation.

---

**Verdict Justification**: ACCEPT-WITH-RESERVATIONS. The implementation is a faithful translation of the design document. The two potential MAJOR findings (threshold typing, missing frameReader guard) both have strong mitigating factors: the threshold typing matches the design's own type choices, and the frameReader guard may be redundant if sendService handles it. All core sections (6 handlers, 2 task builders, wait conditions, protocol adapter, state machine, status timer, connection cleanup, ack frame sending) are present and structurally correct. Review remained in THOROUGH mode -- no escalation to ADVERSARIAL warranted.

**Realist Check**: Finding 1 (threshold type) is mitigated by the fact that the design itself defines `matchValue` and `targetFixedValue` as `string`, and SCOE completion conditions are characteristically string-based comparisons. Finding 2 (missing frameReader guard) is mitigated by the fact that `sendService.execute()` must validate the frameId internally to serialize the frame to bytes.

---

**Open Questions (unscored)**:

1. **`ConditionTerm.threshold` type**: The design uses `string` for `matchValue`/`targetFixedValue`, which the implementation follows. But `ConditionTerm.threshold` accepts `string | number`. Should `buildWaitConditions` ever produce a numeric threshold? If SCOE conditions are always string-based, this is fine. If not, a `Number()` conversion or explicit typing is needed. Requires confirmation from the task-real condition evaluator implementation.

2. **`frameReader.getFrame()` guard in `sendAckFrame`**: The design includes this guard; the implementation omits it. If `sendService.execute()` internally validates frame existence and throws a meaningful error, the guard is unnecessary. If `sendService.execute()` does not validate, a misconfigured `successFrameId` would cause an unhandled exception. Worth confirming by reading `sendService.execute()` internals.

3. **`runtimeSeconds` never reset**: `resetRuntimeState()` does not reset `runtimeSeconds`. This means it tracks total uptime since service creation, not time since last satellite load. If the intent is to track per-session uptime, it should be reset. The design does not list it as a reset field, so the implementation is correct per design, but the design itself may have an oversight.

---

*Summary row: Implementation cross-check against command-ingress design.*
- Section coverage: All 12 design sections verified against implementation
- Handler preconditions: All 6 handlers correct (LOAD: `loadedSatelliteId === ''`, UNLOAD/SEND_FRAME/READ_FILE_AND_SEND: `scoeFramesLoaded`)
- Task builders: Both match design structure (`immediate` / `timer` + `fieldVariations` + `stopCondition`)
- State machine: Two-phase gating + tickSecond 3-step logic matches
- Connection cleanup: Present with tracking map, slightly enhanced beyond design

已确认。`scoe-protocol-adapter.ts` 的第 60 行使用 `commandConfigs.find((c) => c.code === commandCode)`，其中 `commandConfigs` 是在第 23 行捕获的**冻结快照**。LOAD 处理器通过 `stateWriter.setLoaded()` 更新 `activeCommandConfigs`，但适配器从未读取 `stateReader.getSnapshot().activeCommandConfigs`——它使用自己过时的副本。这是一个 CRITICAL 错误。

现在我有了足够的数据来进行完整的综合分析。让我整理我的发现。

---

**VERDICT: REVISE**

**Overall Assessment**: W2 implementation covers most brainstorm decisions but has one CRITICAL bug (command config refresh after LOAD) and several MAJOR gaps in state tracking (lastCommandCode never updated, status timer missing documented behaviors). The architecture and type alignment with task/send/connection features is solid, but the runtime wiring has concrete functional holes.

**Pre-commitment Predictions vs Actual Findings**:

1. **Decision-to-implementation gaps** -- CONFIRMED. Found CRITICAL gap in command config refresh after LOAD (decision 3 area) and MAJOR gap in lastCommandCode tracking (decision 1/2 area).
2. **R1/R2/R3 findings addressed** -- PARTIALLY. R3 "connection disconnect cleanup" was addressed (C18), but R3 "handler precondition gating" for UNLOAD is incomplete (checks `scoeFramesLoaded` instead of `loadedSatelliteId !== ''`).
3. **留空 items** -- CONFIRMED clean. `checkSatelliteLoad` and `sendScoeFrames` were correctly excluded per brainstorm (checklist C17 explicitly says "不含 checkSatelliteLoad/sendScoeFrames").
4. **Edge cases** -- CONFIRMED. Several edge cases unhandled: parse error silently consumed, `onSettled` fire-and-forget without error boundary, READ_FILE_AND_SEND file param detection heuristic.
5. **Checklist satisfaction** -- C12-C16 substantively done, C17 partial, C18 done with limitation.

---

**Critical Findings** (blocks execution):

1. **ScoeProtocolAdapter uses stale commandConfigs after LOAD -- satellite-specific commands unrecognizable**

   - Confidence: **HIGH**
   - Evidence: `scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:23` captures `const commandConfigs = [...options.commandConfigs]` at creation time. `scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:60` uses `commandConfigs.find((c) => c.code === commandCode)` for parsing. But `handle-load.ts:30` calls `ctx.stateWriter.setLoaded(satelliteId, satConfig.commandConfigs)` which only updates `state.activeCommandConfigs` -- the adapter never reads from state. After LOAD, the adapter still uses the initial (pre-satellite) command config list, so non-LOAD commands will fail to parse because their codes won't be found in the stale list.
   - Why this matters: The entire two-phase state machine (brainstorm A21) is broken. LOAD works (code `01` is in initial config), but after LOAD succeeds and `scoeFramesLoaded` becomes `true`, subsequent commands (SEND_FRAME code `05`, etc.) are parsed using the initial config that may not contain satellite-specific command definitions. The old system resolved this by loading frame configurations into the SCOE store; the new system's `activeCommandConfigs` state update is invisible to the adapter.
   - Fix: The adapter's `parse()` function must read `commandConfigs` from `stateReader.getSnapshot().activeCommandConfigs` (or a dedicated state reader method) instead of the closure-captured snapshot. Alternatively, pass a `() => readonly ScoeCommandConfig[]` getter in `ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions` and use it in `parse()`.

---

**Major Findings** (causes significant rework):

1. **`lastCommandCode` is declared in state and selectors but never written by any handler or dispatch path**

   - Confidence: **HIGH**
   - Evidence: Grep across all command-ingress files shows `lastCommandCode` is initialized to `''` in state, exposed in selectors, but **no handler or service ever calls `stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: ... })`**. The brainstorm A18 specifies "统计维护 -- 订阅 task lifecycle event" and A19 says "lastCommandCode / receiveCommandSuccess" are tracked. The old system updated these on every command receive (`scoeStore.ts`).
   - Why this matters: Any UI or diagnostic consuming `selectScoeRuntimeStatus().lastCommandCode` will always see `''`. This defeats observability of which command was last processed.
   - Fix: Add `ctx.stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: command.commandCode })` in either `dispatchCommand()` or the service-level `onCommand` callback in `command-ingress-service.ts:82-88`.

2. **Status timer (`tickSecond`) implements only part of the brainstorm A22 behavior -- missing `checkSatelliteLoad` and `sendScoeFrames` equivalents is correct by design, but `healthStatus`/`linkTestResult` are never updated**

   - Confidence: **HIGH**
   - Evidence: `state.ts:145-169` implements `tickSecond()` with runtimeSeconds++, receiveCommandSuccess reset, and statistics reset when unloaded. This matches brainstorm A22 items 1-4. However, brainstorm A22 mentions `checkSatelliteLoad()` and `sendScoeFrames()` as part of the old timer, and the checklist C17 says "不含 checkSatelliteLoad/sendScoeFrames". That exclusion is deliberate. But the brainstorm also states that `healthStatus` (A18: `unknown/healthy/error`) and `linkTestResult` (A18: `unknown/pass/fail`) are runtime state fields. The handlers for HEALTH_CHECK and LINK_CHECK read snapshots but **never write results back to state** -- `handleHealthCheck.ts` and `handleLinkCheck.ts` just call `ctx.connectionSnapshot()` / `ctx.receiveSnapshot()` and return `{ success: true }` without updating `healthStatus` or `linkTestResult`.
   - Why this matters: `selectScoeRuntimeStatus().healthStatus` will always be `'unknown'` and `linkTestResult` always `'unknown'`. The HEALTH_CHECK and LINK_CHECK commands are supposed to update these fields as a side effect.
   - Fix: HEALTH_CHECK handler should call `ctx.stateWriter.updateStatus({ healthStatus: computedStatus })` based on connection snapshot. LINK_CHECK handler should call `ctx.stateWriter.updateStatus({ linkTestResult: computedResult })` based on receive snapshot. The computation logic (what makes health "healthy" vs "error") needs definition, but at minimum the handlers should write a non-unknown value.

3. **`onSettled()` fire-and-forget in handle-send-frame and handle-read-file-and-send has no error boundary**

   - Confidence: **HIGH**
   - Evidence: `handle-send-frame.ts:17-25` and `handle-read-file-and-send.ts:40-48` both use `ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(() => { ... })` without a `.catch()` handler. If `onSettled` rejects (e.g., task instance cleaned up before settling, or internal task service error), the promise rejection is unhandled.
   - Why this matters: Unhandled promise rejections crash Node.js processes (or surface as uncaught errors in browsers). In the connection disconnect cleanup path (`command-ingress-service.ts:121-127`), `taskService.stopTask()` may settle the task internally, potentially racing with the `onSettled` promise chain.
   - Fix: Add `.catch((err) => { console.error(...); ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(String(err)); })` to the `.then()` chain, or use `void` pattern with try/catch inside an async IIFE.

4. **`handleReadFileAndSend` uses fragile heuristic to find file parameter -- `p.value?.includes('.txt')`**

   - Confidence: **HIGH**
   - Evidence: `handle-read-file-and-send.ts:13` uses `command.commandConfig.params?.find((p) => p.value?.includes('.txt'))` to locate the file parameter. This will miss files with extensions other than `.txt` and would incorrectly match a param whose value happens to contain `.txt` substring (e.g., `output.txt.bak`). The `ScoeCommandParam` type already has `type: 'string' | 'number' | 'boolean'` but no `'file'` type.
   - Why this matters: This is the only mechanism to locate the file parameter for READ_FILE_AND_SEND. Any file extension change or naming pattern variation silently breaks the command.
   - Fix: Either (a) add `type: 'file'` to `ScoeCommandParam.type` and match on that, or (b) add an explicit `role: 'filePath'` discriminator to the param config. The current heuristic is not production-grade.

5. **`cleanupDisconnectedConnections()` is only called after a new command arrives, not proactively on disconnect**

   - Confidence: **HIGH**
   - Evidence: `command-ingress-service.ts:87` calls `cleanupDisconnectedConnections()` only inside `onCommand()` -- i.e., only when a new SCOE command arrives. The brainstorm decision 6 says "监听 connection disconnect event，取消与该连接关联的正在执行的 task." The implementation does not subscribe to connection disconnect events; it only polls connection state when a new command arrives. If a client disconnects and no new command comes in, the associated tasks continue running indefinitely.
   - Why this matters: A TCP client disconnect is the exact scenario that should trigger cleanup. Without proactive monitoring, a disconnected client's SEND_FRAME task continues sending frames until it naturally completes or times out.
   - Fix: Subscribe to connection lifecycle events (or poll at a regular interval, e.g., in the status timer tick) to detect disconnects and stop associated tasks promptly.

---

**Minor Findings** (suboptimal but functional):

1. **`handleUnloadSatelliteId` checks `scoeFramesLoaded` instead of `loadedSatelliteId !== ''`** -- The precondition "No satellite loaded" is semantically about `loadedSatelliteId` being empty, not about `scoeFramesLoaded` being false. In practice they are coupled (set together), but the intent is clearer with the satellite ID check.

2. **`selectCommandIngressSnapshot` manually copies all 13 fields instead of using `structuredClone`** -- Minor code duplication. The function at `command-ingress-selectors.ts:23-40` manually maps every state field, creating maintenance burden when new fields are added. A `structuredClone`-based approach (like `state.ts:75` uses for `getSnapshot`) would be more resilient.

3. **`task-builder.ts:75` uses `userFieldValues` property name which matches `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues` (optional field) but always passes a non-undefined value** -- The field is typed as optional (`userFieldValues?: ...`) in `SendStepConfig`, and the task builder always provides it. This works but is worth noting for consistency.

4. **No test for `sendAckFrame` directly** -- The `send-ack-frame.ts` module is tested only indirectly through handler tests. Given its role in the response mechanism (brainstorm decision 5), direct unit tests would strengthen coverage.

5. **`ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions.onCommand` is optional** (`onCommand?:`) but if it is not provided, the adapter silently consumes events without dispatching. This is technically correct for W1 stub usage but could mask wiring bugs.

---

**What's Missing** (gaps, unhandled edge cases, unstated assumptions):

- **Command config refresh mechanism**: No documented mechanism for how the adapter's `commandConfigs` get updated after LOAD. The state stores `activeCommandConfigs` but nothing connects it to the adapter's parse logic.
- **HEALTH_CHECK / LINK_CHECK response data**: The handlers return `{ success: true }` but don't include the actual health/link data in the result. The brainstorm says "handler 返回 snapshot" but the `CommandResult` type has no field for snapshot data. The handler captures the snapshot internally (side-effect only) but doesn't return it.
- **Parse error telemetry**: `scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:97` logs `console.warn` on parse errors but doesn't update any state counter. Brainstorm A18 says "统计维护" includes error counts for failed commands, but parse failures bypass the handler dispatch entirely and thus bypass `incrementErrorCount`.
- **`activeCommandConfigs` lifecycle**: After UNLOAD, `activeCommandConfigs` is cleared (in `resetRuntimeState`). But the adapter still holds the old snapshot in its closure. Re-loading a different satellite would give different command configs to state but not to the adapter.
- **`sendAckFrame` UDP target selection**: The ack frame uses `command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId` as fallback target. For commands without frame mappings (LOAD, HEALTH_CHECK, LINK_CHECK), this would be undefined, falling back to `globalConfig.udpTargetId`. This is correct but the ack frame is only sent from SEND_FRAME and READ_FILE_AND_SEND handlers (both have frame mappings), making the fallback for non-mapping commands dead code.

---

**Ambiguity Risks**:

- brainstorm says `"adapter 在 taskService.onSettled() 成功后通过 connection.write() 发送 successFrameId 帧"` but implementation uses `sendService.execute()` instead of `connection.write()`. This is likely a correct design evolution (sendService wraps connection.write and handles frame serialization), but the brainstorm specifically mentioned `connection.write()` while the design/checklist says "通过 sendService.execute() 发送确认帧". No conflict in intent, just wording.
- brainstorm A22 `statusUpdate` timer item 5 says `"调用 checkSatelliteLoad() 和 sendScoeFrames()"` and checklist C17 says "不含 checkSatelliteLoad/sendScoeFrames". This is an explicit exclusion documented in the checklist, so it is not a gap -- it is a deliberate scope cut.

---

**Multi-Perspective Notes**:

- **Executor**: "The CRITICAL config-refresh bug would stop me at LOAD + SEND_FRAME integration testing. After implementing C12, I'd write a test that does LOAD then SEND_FRAME and immediately discover that the adapter can't find the command code. Also, the file param heuristic in READ_FILE_AND_SEND would make me ask 'what if the file is .csv or has no extension?'"
- **Stakeholder**: "The observability story is broken. `lastCommandCode` never updates, `healthStatus` stays `'unknown'`, `linkTestResult` stays `'unknown'`. If the UI shows SCOE status, it will always look like nothing has happened."
- **Skeptic**: "The biggest risk is the fire-and-forget onSettled pattern. It means command-ingress loses control of the task lifecycle after creation. If the task service has a bug or the task gets stopped externally, the ack frame logic silently fails with no diagnostic trail."

---

**Verdict Justification**:

REVISE because of one CRITICAL and five MAJOR findings. The CRITICAL finding (stale command configs after LOAD) breaks the core two-phase state machine that the brainstorm specifically identified as a "CRITICAL-3" finding from the old system. This would be caught in the very first integration test.

I did not escalate to ADVERSARIAL mode because the codebase structure is sound -- types align correctly with task/send/connection public APIs, the handler map pattern matches the brainstorm decision 2, selector immutability is correct, and the architecture boundaries are properly maintained. The issues are all in the wiring and runtime logic, not in the structural design.

Realist check: The CRITICAL finding survives because it breaks a documented core behavior (LOAD then SEND). MAJOR #2 (healthStatus/linkTestResult never updated) would be downgraded if these handlers are intentionally stub-like pending UI integration, but the brainstorm explicitly listed them as covered behaviors (A11, A12), so the downgrade is not justified. MAJOR #5 (proactive disconnect cleanup) is mitigated by the fact that tasks have natural timeouts and stop conditions, but the brainstorm specifically called this out as a new improvement over the old system, so it should be implemented as designed.

---

**Open Questions (unscored)**:

- Is the brainstorm A22 item 5 (`checkSatelliteLoad`, `sendScoeFrames`) truly "not needed" or just "deferred to W3"? The checklist C17 excludes them, but the brainstorm describes them as part of the "每秒执行的运行时状态机". If deferred, this should be tracked.
- The brainstorm mentions `"待外部确认 -- runtimeSeconds 的更新方式（定时回调 vs task event 驱动）"`. The implementation chose `setInterval` (timer callback). Was this confirmed with the project owner?
- `CommandContext` has `frameReader: FrameAssetReader` but it is never used in any handler or task-builder. Brainstorm input #7 says "帧定义只读查询" and decision 5 mentions using `frameReader.getFrame()` for ack frames, but `sendAckFrame` uses `sendService.execute()` directly without querying frame definitions. Is `frameReader` in the context needed at all, or is it for future use?

---

*Summary row (not a ralplan review, but checking brainstorm-to-implementation consistency)*:
- Decision Implementation: **PARTIAL** -- 8 of 10 decisions implemented correctly; Decision 3 (LOAD/UNLOAD flow) has a config-refresh gap; Decision 6 (disconnect cleanup) is reactive-only
- R1/R2/R3 Self-check Coverage: **PARTIAL** -- R3 "handler precondition gating" is implemented but UNLOAD precondition check is semantically imprecise; R3 "connection disconnect cleanup" exists but is not proactive
- Checklist C12-C18: **C12 PASS, C13 PASS, C14 PASS, C15 PASS, C16 PASS, C17 PARTIAL (tickSecond logic complete but missing healthStatus/linkTestResult update mechanism), C18 PARTIAL (tracking map exists but cleanup is not event-driven)**

## 代码审查总结

**审查文件数：** 12
**总问题数：** 10

### 严重性分布
- 致命 (CRITICAL): 1 (必须修复)
- 高 (HIGH): 3 (应修复)
- 中 (MEDIUM): 4 (考虑修复)

### 问题

**[致命] 未处理的 promise 拒绝：`onSettled().then()` 没有 `.catch()`**
文件：`rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/handle-send-frame.ts:17-25`
文件：`rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/handle-read-file-and-send.ts:40-48`

`onSettled` 回调链使用了 `.then()`，但没有 `.catch()`。如果 `.then()` 内部的任何代码抛出异常（例如，`sendAckFrame` 向网络发送失败，`sendService.execute` 拒绝，或 `ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount()` 抛出异常），这将导致未处理的 promise 拒绝。在 `Node.js`/`Electron` 中，未处理的 promise 拒绝可能会使进程崩溃。

```typescript
// handle-send-frame.ts:17-25 — 当前
ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(() => {
  const inst = ctx.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
  if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
    sendAckFrame(command, ctx);  // <-- 可以抛出 / 拒绝 (async)
    ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
  } else {
    ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');
  }
});
// 没有 .catch() — 如果任何内部操作抛出或 sendAckFrame 拒绝，
// promise 将被未处理地拒绝
```

修复：添加 `.catch()` 并包含 `sendAckFrame` 调用。此外，`sendAckFrame` 返回 `Promise<void>`，但未在 `.then()` 内部 `await`，因此其拒绝也会逸出。

```typescript
ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(async () => {
  const inst = ctx.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
  if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
    try {
      await sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
    } catch { /* ack 发送失败 — 记录或计数器 */ }
    ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
  } else {
    ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');
  }
}).catch((err) => {
  ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(
    err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'settlement tracking failed'
  );
});
```

---

**[高] `trackTask` 在 `.then()` 中使用了陈旧的 `Set` 引用 — `dispose` 后存在潜在竞态条件**
文件：`rewrite/src/services/command-ingress-service.ts:109-114`

```typescript
function trackTask(connectionId: string, taskId: string): void {
  let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
  if (!set) {
    set = new Set();
    trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);
  }
  set.add(taskId);

  taskService.onSettled(taskId).then(() => {
    set!.delete(taskId);        // <-- 'set' 在闭包创建时被捕获
    if (set!.size === 0) {
      trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
    }
  });
}
```

如果 `dispose()` 在 `onSettled` 解析之前运行，`trackingMap.clear()` 会清除 map，但 `.then()` 闭包仍持有对 `set` 的引用。当 `.then()` 触发时，它会调用 `set!.delete(taskId)` — 这会成功（操作已分离的 `Set`），然后调用 `trackingMap.delete(connectionId)`，但此时 `trackingMap` 中已不存在该 `connectionId`（它之前已被 `clear()` 清除）。这虽然无害，但表明 `.then()` 回调可能在服务已销毁后运行。

更严重的问题是：如果在同一个 `connectionId` 上快速发出多个命令，并且第一个命令的 `onSettled` 在第二个 `trackTask` 为该连接创建一个新的 `Set` *之后*才解析，那么第一个 `.then()` 可能会错误地 `delete` 第二个命令正在使用的 `connectionId` 条目。

修复：在 `.then()` 闭包内部，重新查找 `trackingMap` 中的 `Set`，而不是使用捕获的引用：

```typescript
taskService.onSettled(taskId).then(() => {
  const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
  if (!current) return; // 已清理
  current.delete(taskId);
  if (current.size === 0) {
    trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
  }
});
```

---

**[高] `buildReadFileAndSendTask` 为 `send` 步骤发出空的 `userFieldValues` — 文件行只存在于 `fieldVariations` 中，但它们映射到哪个字段？**
文件：`rewrite/src/core/task-builder.ts:132-139`

```typescript
steps.push({
  id: 'send-rfs',
  kind: 'send',
  config: {
    frameId: mapping.frameId,
    targetId: mapping.targetId,
    userFieldValues: {},  // <-- 始终为空
  },
});
```

`fileFieldMapping` 是通过查找 `source === 'param'` 且 `paramId === fileParam?.id` 的参数来确定的，但基础 `send` 步骤的 `userFieldValues` 为空。`fieldVariations` 为 `fileFieldMapping.fieldId` 提供了不同的值。这在功能上意味着 `send` 步骤从无字段值开始，然后 `task` 引擎从每次迭代的 `fieldVariations` 中注入。请验证 `task` 引擎确实将 `fieldVariations` 合并到步骤的 `userFieldValues` 中 — 如果没有，文件数据将永远不会进入发送的帧中。

修复：要么确认 `task` 引擎的 `fieldVariations` 合并行为是正确的，要么在基础步骤的 `userFieldValues` 中为非文件字段填充（类似于 `buildSendFrameTask` 的做法）。添加注释解释为什么在这里空值是正确的。

---

**[高] 脆弱的文件参数检测：`.includes('.txt')` 是启发式方法，而非契约**
文件：`rewrite/src/core/task-builder.ts:124`
文件：`rewrite/src/handlers/handle-read-file-and-send.ts:12`

```typescript
const fileParam = config.params?.find((p) => p.value?.includes('.txt'));
```

这将文件参数识别为“其默认 `value` 包含 `.txt` 的任何参数”。这很脆弱——如果一个参数碰巧有一个包含 `.txt` 的描述性标签，或者如果文件扩展名发生变化（例如 `.csv`、`.bin`），这个检测就会静默失败。`task-builder.ts` 和 `handler` 都重复了这种启发式方法。

修复：向 `ScoeCommandParam` 添加一个 `role` 鉴别器（例如，`role?: 'file-path'`），并使用它代替字符串包含检查。至少，将启发式方法提取到一个共享函数中，这样它就不会被重复：

```typescript
// shared util
export function findFileParam(params?: readonly ScoeCommandParam[]): ScoeCommandParam | undefined {
  return params?.find((p) => p.value?.includes('.txt'));
}
```

**[中] `handle-send-frame.ts` 和 `handle-read-file-and-send.ts` 之间重复的 `onSettled` 监控模式**
文件：`rewrite/src/handlers/handle-send-frame.ts:17-25`
文件：`rewrite/src/handlers/handle-read-file-and-send.ts:40-48`

两个 `handler` 都有相同的样板代码：

```typescript
ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(() => {
  const inst = ctx.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
  if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
    sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
    ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
  } else {
    ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');
  }
});
```

这违反了 `DRY` 原则。如果一个 `handler` 修复了其 `.catch()` 而另一个没有，这也很脆弱。此外，`command-ingress-service.ts:109-114` 中的 `command-ingress-service.ts` 中的 `trackTask` 也为相同目的调用了 `onSettled`——同一个任务有两个独立的 `onSettled` 订阅。

修复：将结算监控（包括 `sendAckFrame` 和计数器更新）提取到 `handler` 和服务都可以使用的共享函数中。`service` 层的 `trackTask` 应该处理 `cleanup`，`handler` 层应该处理 `ack` 和计数器——或者将其统一到一处。

---

**[中] `sendAckFrame` 的 `targetId` 回退链可能无法解析到正确的目标**
文件：`rewrite/src/handlers/send-ack-frame.ts:21-23`

```typescript
targetId:
  command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId ??
  ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,
```

这会回退到全局 `udpTargetId`。如果 `READ_FILE_AND_SEND` 或 `SEND_FRAME` 命令有多个帧映射，其中第一个映射的 `targetId` 为空，这将静默地发送到 `UDP` 目标。问题在于，发出命令的连接的 `targetId` 可能不是 `UDP` 目标。此外，`??`（`nullish coalescing`，空值合并运算符）只检查 `null`/`undefined`，而不是空字符串——如果 `frameMappings[0].targetId` 是 `''`，它将不会被回退捕获。

修复：也检查空字符串：

```typescript
targetId:
  command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId ||
  ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,
```

---

**[中] `state.ts` 中 `tickSecond()` 在卫星未加载时重置计数器 — 可能导致计数器意外清零**
文件：`rewrite/src/core/state.ts:146-160`

```typescript
tickSecond() {
  let next = {
    ...state,
    runtimeSeconds: state.runtimeSeconds + 1,
    receiveCommandSuccess: false,
  };

  if (!next.scoeFramesLoaded) {
    next = {
      ...next,
      commandReceiveCount: 0,
      commandSuccessCount: 0,
      commandErrorCount: 0,
      healthStatus: 'unknown',
      linkTestResult: 'unknown',
    };
  }
```

每一秒，如果卫星未加载，计数器都会重置为 0。这意味着如果在卸载卫星和重新加载之间有命令到达（虽然不太可能但有可能），计数器将每秒被清零。这是一种主动擦除状态的行为，在定时器驱动的 `tick` 中可能不符合预期。

修复：考虑只在 `resetRuntimeState()` 中重置计数器，而不是在每个 `tick` 上。或者至少添加一条注释解释为什么每秒清零是故意的（例如，计数器是“已加载”会话的“每秒速率”）。

---

**[中] `command-ingress-service.ts` 中 `onCommand` 抛出异常 — 适配器的 `consume()` 会静默吞噬该异常**
文件：`rewrite/src/services/command-ingress-service.ts:82-88`
文件：`rewrite/src/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:91-98`

在 `service` 中：

```typescript
async function onCommand(parsed): Promise<void> {
  const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);
  if (result.taskId) {
    trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId);
  }
  cleanupDisconnectedConnections();
}
```

在 `adapter` 中：

```typescript
try {
  const parsed = parse(event);
  if (onCommand) {
    await onCommand(parsed);
  }
} catch (err) {
  console.warn(`SCOE parse error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
}
```

如果 `onCommand` 抛出异常（例如，`trackTask` 遇到问题，`cleanupDisconnectedConnections` 失败），错误将被捕获为“`SCOE` 解析错误”——这具有误导性，因为解析成功，但 `dispatch` 失败。该事件仍被标记为 `consumed`，因此命令会悄无声息地丢失。

修复：将捕获日志区分为：

```typescript
} catch (err) {
  console.warn(`SCOE command error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
}
```

或者将解析错误与 `dispatch` 错误分开处理。

---

**[低] `handle-health-check.ts` 捕获连接快照但从不使用它**
文件：`rewrite/src/handlers/handle-health-check.ts:5`

```typescript
ctx.connectionSnapshot();
return { success: true };
```

连接快照被捕获但返回值被丢弃。如果意图是根据连接状态设置 `healthStatus`，则缺少该逻辑。当前代码成功，但除了可能的副作用外没有可观察到的效果。

修复：要么使用快照来更新 `healthStatus`，要么删除死调用。添加注释解释预期行为。

---

**[低] `scoe-protocol-adapter.ts` 中 `console.warn` 用于错误日志记录 — 与基于 `service` 层模式不一致**
文件：`rewrite/src/adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter.ts:97`

```typescript
console.warn(`SCOE parse error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
```

代码库中的其他 `service` 模式不使用 `console.warn`。考虑通过可配置的 `logger` 接口或状态写入器（例如，`incrementErrorCount`）路由错误，以保持一致性并支持测试。

修复：接受 `options` 中一个可选的 `onError` 回调，或者写入一个共享的 `logger` 抽象。短期内，这是可以接受的，但应标记为技术债务。

---

### 积极观察

-   **清晰的依赖方向：** `handlers/` 只从 `core/` 导入，`core/` 从不导入 `handlers/`。没有循环依赖风险。架构边界维护得很好。
-   **关注点分离得当：** `Protocol adapter`（字节解析）、`handler`（命令分派）、`service`（生命周期/跟踪）和 `state`（不可变快照）具有清晰的职责。这遵循了其他 `feature`（`send`、`receive`、`task`）中使用的相同 `core/services/adapters` 分层。
-   **类型安全的 `CommandContext` 接口：** 依赖项通过良好定义的接口而不是具体实现注入，使代码易于测试。
-   **`state.ts` 中不可变状态处理：** `getSnapshot()` 使用 `structuredClone`，并且所有写入操作都会创建新的状态对象，遵循项目的 `selector` 不可变性规则。
-   **`handler` 工厂模式（`createHandleLoadSatelliteId`）：** 使用闭包而不是全局变量或模块状态注入配置是一个清晰的模式，其他只读 `handler` 则使用简单的 `const` 导出。
-   **`TypeScript` 通过 `tsc --noEmit`：** 没有类型错误。

---

### 建议

**需要修改** — 致命（`onSettled` 上未处理的 `promise` 拒绝）必须先修复。`Set` 引用过期的竞态条件和重复的 `onSettled` 监控也应在此次合并前解决。

在致命（`CRITICAL`）和高（`HIGH`）问题解决后，此实现可以准备就绪。中（`MEDIUM`）和低（`LOW`）问题可以在后续工作中解决。

1	import type { ProtocolAdapter, ParsedCommand } from '../core';
2	import type {
3	  ScoeGlobalConfig,
4	  ScoeCommandConfig,
5	  ChecksumConfig,
6	  ScoeCommandParam,
7	} from '../core';
8	import type { CommandIngressStateReader } from '../core';
9	import type { TransportEventSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
10	import type { ConsumerResult } from '@/runtime/consumer-chain';
11	
12	export interface ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions {
13	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
14	  readonly commandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
15	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
16	  readonly onCommand?: (parsed: ParsedCommand) => Promise<void>;
17	}
18	
19	export function createScoeProtocolAdapter(
20	  options: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions,
21	): ProtocolAdapter {
22	  const { globalConfig, stateReader, onCommand } = options;
23	  const commandConfigs = [...options.commandConfigs];
24	
25	  function canHandle(event: TransportEventSnapshot): boolean {
26	    if (event.kind !== 'data' || event.bytes === undefined) return false;
27	
28	    const bytes = event.bytes;
29	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
30	
31	    if (bytes.length < fcOffset + 4) return false;
32	
33	    const identifierByte = bytes[fcOffset];
34	    const expectedIdentifier = hexToByte(globalConfig.scoeIdentifier);
35	    if (expectedIdentifier === null || identifierByte !== expectedIdentifier) return false;
36	
37	    // Fixed 0xAA 0xAA at offset +2 and +3
38	    if (bytes[fcOffset + 2] !== 0xaa || bytes[fcOffset + 3] !== 0xaa) return false;
39	
40	    // Two-phase state machine
41	    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
42	      // Phase 1: only match LOAD_SATELLITE_ID (command code 01)
43	      const commandCode = byteToHex(bytes[fcOffset + 1]!);
44	      if (commandCode !== '01') return false;
45	    }
46	
47	    return true;
48	  }
49	
50	  function parse(event: TransportEventSnapshot): ParsedCommand {
51	    if (event.bytes === undefined) throw new Error('Event has no bytes');
52	
53	    const bytes = event.bytes;
54	    const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
55	
56	    const codeByte = bytes[fcOffset + 1];
57	    if (codeByte === undefined) throw new Error('Command code byte missing');
58	
59	    const commandCode = byteToHex(codeByte);
60	    const commandConfig = commandConfigs.find((c) => c.code === commandCode);
61	    if (!commandConfig) {
62	      throw new Error(`Unknown SCOE command code: ${commandCode}`);
63	    }
64	
65	    validateChecksums(bytes, commandConfig.checksums);
66	
67	    const resolvedParams = extractAndResolveParams(bytes, commandConfig.params ?? []);
68	
69	    return {
70	      commandId: `scoe-cmd-${event.id}`,
71	      commandCode,
72	      commandFunction: commandConfig.function,
73	      rawBytes: [...bytes],
74	      resolvedParams,
75	      commandConfig,
76	      connectionId: event.connectionId,
77	      occurredAt: event.occurredAt,
78	    };
79	  }
80	
81	  return {
82	    id: 'scoe-protocol',
83	    protocolId: 'scoe-tcp',
84	
85	    async consume(events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[]): Promise<ConsumerResult> {
86	      const consumed: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
87	      const remaining: TransportEventSnapshot[] = [];
88	
89	      for (const event of events) {
90	        if (canHandle(event)) {
91	          try {
92	            const parsed = parse(event);
93	            if (onCommand) {
94	              await onCommand(parsed);
95	            }
96	          } catch (err) {
97	            console.warn(`SCOE parse error: ${(err as Error).message}`);
98	          }
99	          consumed.push(event);
100	        } else {
101	          remaining.push(event);
102	        }
103	      }
104	
105	      return { consumed, remaining };
106	    },
107	  };
108	}
109	
110	// --- Byte-level helpers (exported for testing) ---
111	
112	export function byteToHex(byte: number): string {
113	  return byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase();
114	}
115	
116	export function hexToByte(hex: string): number | null {
117	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '');
118	  const value = parseInt(cleaned, 16);
119	  return Number.isNaN(value) ? null : value;
120	}
121	
122	export function validateChecksums(
123	  bytes: readonly number[],
124	  checksums: readonly ChecksumConfig[],
125	): void {
126	  for (const cs of checksums) {
127	    if (!cs.enabled) continue;
128	
129	    let sum = 0;
130	    for (let i = cs.offset; i < cs.offset + cs.length; i++) {
131	      sum += bytes[i] ?? 0;
132	    }
133	    const expected = sum % 256;
134	    const actual = bytes[cs.checksumOffset] ?? 0;
135	
136	    if (expected !== actual) {
137	      throw new Error(
138	        `Checksum failed at offset ${cs.checksumOffset}: expected ${expected}, got ${actual}`,
139	      );
140	    }
141	  }
142	}
143	
144	export function extractAndResolveParams(
145	  bytes: readonly number[],
146	  params: readonly ScoeCommandParam[],
147	): Record<string, string> {
148	  const result: Record<string, string> = {};
149	
150	  for (const param of params) {
151	    const slice = bytes.slice(param.offset, param.offset + param.length);
152	    const hex = bytesToUpperCaseHex(slice);
153	    const normalized = normalizeHex(hex, param.length * 2);
154	
155	    const matched = param.options.find(
156	      (opt) => normalizeHex(opt.receiveCode, param.length * 2) === normalized,
157	    );
158	
159	    result[param.id] = matched?.value ?? hex;
160	  }
161	
162	  return result;
163	}
164	
165	function bytesToUpperCaseHex(bytes: readonly number[]): string {
166	  return Array.from(bytes)
167	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase())
168	    .join('');
169	}
170	
171	function normalizeHex(hex: string, targetLength: number): string {
172	  const cleaned = hex.replace(/^0x/i, '').toUpperCase();
173	  return cleaned.padStart(targetLength, '0');
174	}
175	
```

1	import type { ParsedCommand } from '../core/protocol-adapter';
2	import type { CommandContext } from '../core/handler';
3	
4	/**
5	 * Send acknowledgment frame via sendService after task completes successfully.
6	 * Uses successFrameId from command config (falling back to global config),
7	 * resolved through sendService.execute() which handles frame serialization
8	 * and connection write.
9	 */
10	export async function sendAckFrame(
11	  command: ParsedCommand,
12	  ctx: CommandContext,
13	): Promise<void> {
14	  const successFrameId =
15	    command.commandConfig.successFrameId ??
16	    ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().successFrameId;
17	  if (!successFrameId) return;
18	
19	  await ctx.sendService.execute({
20	    frameId: successFrameId,
21	    targetId:
22	      command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId ??
23	      ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,
24	    userFieldValues: {},
25	  });
26	}
27
