1	import type { TaskService } from '@/features/task';
2	import type { SendService } from '@/features/send';
3	import type { ConnectionService, ConnectionStateSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
4	import type { FrameAssetReader } from '@/features/frame';
5	import type { ProtocolAdapter } from '../core/protocol-adapter';
6	import type { CommandContext, CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';
7	import type { ScoeCommandFunction, SatelliteConfig } from '../core/types';
8	import type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from '../core/state';
9	import type { ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';
10	import { dispatchCommand } from '../core/handler';
11	import { createScoeProtocolAdapter } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';
12	import { createHandleLoadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-load';
13	import { handleUnloadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-unload';
14	import { handleHealthCheck } from '../handlers/handle-health-check';
15	import { handleLinkCheck } from '../handlers/handle-link-check';
16	import { handleSendFrame } from '../handlers/handle-send-frame';
17	import { handleReadFileAndSend } from '../handlers/handle-read-file-and-send';
18	import { startStatusTimer } from './status-timer';
19	
20	export interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {
21	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];
22	  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];
23	  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];
24	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
25	  readonly sendService: SendService;
26	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
27	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
28	  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;
29	  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;
30	  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;
31	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
32	  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;
33	}
34	
35	export interface CommandIngressService {
36	  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;
37	  dispose(): void;
38	}
39	
40	export function createCommandIngressService(
41	  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,
42	): CommandIngressService {
43	  const {
44	    satelliteConfigs,
45	    taskService,
46	    sendService,
47	    frameReader,
48	    connectionService,
49	    connectionSnapshot,
50	    receiveSnapshot,
51	    platformFileReader,
52	    stateReader,
53	    stateWriter,
54	  } = options;
55	
56	  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds
57	  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
58	
59	  // Handler map
60	  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([
61	    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],
62	    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],
63	    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],
64	    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],
65	    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],
66	    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],
67	  ]);
68	
69	  const ctx: CommandContext = {
70	    taskService,
71	    sendService,
72	    frameReader,
73	    connectionService,
74	    connectionSnapshot,
75	    receiveSnapshot,
76	    platformFileReader,
77	    stateReader,
78	    stateWriter,
79	  };
80	
81	  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands to handlers
82	  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {
83	    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);
84	    if (result.taskId) {
85	      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId);
86	    }
87	    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();
88	  }
89	
90	  // Create protocol adapter with onCommand dispatch
91	  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({
92	    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,
93	    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,
94	    stateReader,
95	    onCommand,
96	  });
97	
98	  // Start status timer
99	  const stopTimer = startStatusTimer(stateWriter);
100	
101	  function trackTask(connectionId: string, taskId: string): void {
102	    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
103	    if (!set) {
104	      set = new Set();
105	      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);
106	    }
107	    set.add(taskId);
108	
109	    taskService.onSettled(taskId).then(() => {
110	      set!.delete(taskId);
111	      if (set!.size === 0) {
112	        trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
113	      }
114	    });
115	  }
116	
117	  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {
118	    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
119	      const fact = connectionService.getConnectionFact(connId);
120	      if (!fact || fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected') {
121	        for (const taskId of taskIds) {
122	          try {
123	            taskService.stopTask(taskId);
124	          } catch {
125	            // Task may already be settled
126	          }
127	        }
128	        trackingMap.delete(connId);
129	      }
130	    }
131	  }
132	
133	  function dispose(): void {
134	    stopTimer();
135	    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
136	      for (const taskId of taskIds) {
137	        try {
138	          taskService.stopTask(taskId);
139	        } catch {
140	          // Ignore errors during cleanup
141	        }
142	      }
143	    }
144	    trackingMap.clear();
145	  }
146	
147	  return {
148	    adapter,
149	    dispose,
150	  };
151	}
152	
```

1	import type { CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';

5	export const handleReadFileAndSend: CommandHandler = async (command, ctx) => {
6	  if (!ctx.stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
7	    return { success: false, error: 'Satellite not loaded' };
8	  }
9	
10	  // Find the file path from command params
11	  const fileParam = command.commandConfig.params?.find(
12	    (p) => p.value?.includes('.txt'),
13	  );
14	  if (!fileParam) {
15	    return { success: false, error: 'No file parameter found' };
16	  }
17	
18	  const filePath = command.resolvedParams[fileParam.id] ?? fileParam.value;
19	  let fileLines: string[];
20	  try {
21	    fileLines = await ctx.platformFileReader(filePath);
22	  } catch (err) {
23	    return {
24	      success: false,
25	      error: `Failed to read file: ${(err as Error).message}`,
26	    };
27	  }
28	
29	  if (fileLines.length === 0) {
30	    return { success: false, error: 'File is empty' };
31	  }
32	
33	  const taskDef = buildReadFileAndSendTask(command, fileLines);
34	  const instance = ctx.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
35	  ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
36	
37	  ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount();
38	
39	  // Fire-and-forget: monitor task settlement for ack frame
40	  ctx.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId).then(() => {
41	    const inst = ctx.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
42	    if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
43	      sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
44	      ctx.stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
45	    } else {
46	      ctx.stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');
47	    }
48	  });
49	
50	  return { success: true, taskId: instance.instanceId };
51	};
52	
```

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/send-ack-frame.ts",
  "content": "import type { ParsedCommand } from '../core/protocol-adapter';\nimport type { CommandContext } from '../core/handler';\n\n/**\n * Send acknowledgment frame via sendService after task completes successfully.\n * Uses successFrameId from command config (falling back to global config),\n * resolved through sendService.execute() which handles frame serialization\n * and connection write.\n */\nexport async function sendAckFrame(\n  command: ParsedCommand,\n  ctx: CommandContext,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const successFrameId =\n    command.commandConfig.successFrameId ??\n    ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().successFrameId;\n  if (!successFrameId) return;\n\n  const frame = ctx.frameReader.getFrame(successFrameId);\n  if (!frame) return;\n\n  await ctx.sendService.execute({\n    frameId: successFrameId,\n    targetId:\n      command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId ||\n      ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,\n    userFieldValues: {},\n  });\n}\n"
}
```

}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/handlers/handle-read-file-and-send.ts",
  "content": "import type { CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';\nimport { buildReadFileAndSendTask } from '../core/task-builder';\n\nexport const handleReadFileAndSend: CommandHandler = async (command, ctx) => {\n  if (!ctx.stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {\n    return { success: false, error: 'Satellite not loaded' };\n  }\n\n  // Find the file path from command params\n  const fileParam = command.commandConfig.params?.find(\n    (p) => p.value?.includes('.txt'),\n  );\n  if (!fileParam) {\n    return { success: false, error: 'No file parameter found' };\n  }\n\n  const filePath = command.resolvedParams[fileParam.id] ?? fileParam.value;\n  let fileLines: string[];\n  try {\n    fileLines = await ctx.platformFileReader(filePath);\n  } catch (err) {\n    return {\n      success: false,\n      error: `Failed to read file: ${(err as Error).message}`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (fileLines.length === 0) {\n    return { success: false, error: 'File is empty' };\n  }\n\n  const taskDef = buildReadFileAndSendTask(command, fileLines);\n  const instance = ctx.taskService.createTask(taskDef);\n  ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);\n\n  ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount();\n\n  return { success: true, taskId: instance.instanceId };\n};\n"
}

  "content": "import type { CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';\n\nexport const handleHealthCheck: CommandHandler = async (_command, ctx) => {\n  const snapshot = ctx.connectionSnapshot();\n  // Determine health from connection lifecycle status\n  const hasConnected = Array.isArray((snapshot as any)?.runtimeFacts)\n    ? (snapshot as any).runtimeFacts.some(\n        (f: any) => f.lifecycle === 'connected',\n      )\n    : false;\n  ctx.stateWriter.updateStatus({\n    healthStatus: hasConnected ? 'healthy' : 'error',\n  });\n  return { success: true };\n};\n"
}

'pass' : 'fail',\n  });\n  return { success: true };\n};\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/command-ingress/services/command-ingress-service.ts",
  "content": "import type { TaskService } from '@/features/task';\nimport type { SendService } from '@/features/send';\nimport type { ConnectionService, ConnectionStateSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';\nimport type { FrameAssetReader } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type { ProtocolAdapter } from '../core/protocol-adapter';\nimport type { CommandContext, CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';\nimport type { ScoeCommandFunction, SatelliteConfig } from '../core/types';\nimport type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from '../core/state';\nimport type { ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';\nimport { dispatchCommand } from '../core/handler';\nimport { createScoeProtocolAdapter } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';\nimport { createHandleLoadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-load';\nimport { handleUnloadSatelliteId } from '../handlers/handle-unload';\nimport { handleHealthCheck } from '../handlers/handle-health-check';\nimport { handleLinkCheck } from '../handlers/handle-link-check';\nimport { handleSendFrame } from '../handlers/handle-send-frame';\nimport { handleReadFileAndSend } from '../handlers/handle-read-file-and-send';\nimport { sendAckFrame } from '../handlers/send-ack-frame';\nimport { startStatusTimer } from './status-timer';\n\nexport interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {\n  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];\n  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];\n  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];\n  readonly taskService: TaskService;\n  readonly sendService: SendService;\n  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;\n  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;\n  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;\n  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;\n  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;\n  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;\n  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;\n}\n\nexport interface CommandIngressService {\n  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;\n  dispose(): void;\n}\n\nexport function createCommandIngressService(\n  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,\n): CommandIngressService {\n  const {\n    satelliteConfigs,\n    taskService,\n    sendService,\n    frameReader,\n    connectionService,\n    connectionSnapshot,\n    receiveSnapshot,\n    platformFileReader,\n    stateReader,\n    stateWriter,\n  } = options;\n\n  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds\n  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();\n\n  // Handler map\n  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([\n    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],\n    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],\n    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],\n    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],\n    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],\n    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],\n  ]);\n\n  const ctx: CommandContext = {\n    taskService,\n    sendService,\n    frameReader,\n    connectionService,\n    connectionSnapshot,\n    receiveSnapshot,\n    platformFileReader,\n    stateReader,\n    stateWriter,\n  };\n\n  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands, track tasks, monitor settlement\n  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {\n    stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: parsed.commandCode });\n\n    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);\n\n    if (result.taskId) {\n      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId, parsed);\n    }\n\n    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();\n  }\n\n  // Create protocol adapter with onCommand dispatch\n  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,\n    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,\n    stateReader,\n    onCommand,\n  });\n\n  // Start status timer\n  const stopTimer = startStatusTimer(stateWriter);\n\n  function trackTask(\n    connectionId: string,\n    taskId: string,\n    command: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0],\n  ): void {\n    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);\n    if (!set) {\n      set = new Set();\n      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);\n    }\n    set.add(taskId);\n\n    // Single consolidated settlement monitor: cleanup tracking + ack frame + counters\n    taskService.onSettled(taskId)\n      .then(async () => {\n        const inst = taskService.getInstance(taskId);\n        if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {\n          try {\n            await sendAckFrame(command, ctx);\n          } catch {\n            // Ack frame send failure — don't crash, just count as error\n          }\n          stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();\n        } else {\n          stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');\n        }\n\n        // Re-lookup Set from trackingMap (avoids stale closure reference)\n        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);\n        if (!current) return;\n        current.delete(taskId);\n        if (current.size === 0) {\n          trackingMap.delete(connectionId);\n        }\n      })\n      .catch((err) => {\n        stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(\n          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'settlement tracking failed',\n        );\n        // Still cleanup tracking\n        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);\n        if (current) {\n          current.delete(taskId);\n          if (current.size === 0) trackingMap.delete(connectionId);\n        }\n      });\n  }\n\n  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {\n    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {\n      const fact = connectionService.getConnectionFact(connId);\n      if (!fact || fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected') {\n        for (const taskId of taskIds) {\n          try {\n            taskService.stopTask(taskId);\n          } catch {\n            // Task may already be settled\n          }\n        }\n        trackingMap.delete(connId);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  function dispose(): void {\n    stopTimer();\n    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {\n      for (const taskId of taskIds) {\n        try {\n          taskService.stopTask(taskId);\n        } catch {\n          // Ignore errors during cleanup\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    trackingMap.clear();\n  }\n\n  return {\n    adapter,\n    dispose,\n  };\n}\n"
}

Build 通过，57 tests 全绿。

**审查修复总结：**

| 问题 | 严重度 | 修复 |
|---|---|---|
| adapter commandConfigs 不刷新（LOAD 后无法解析卫星命令） | CRITICAL | `parse()` 改用 `getCommandConfigs()` 从 state 动态读取 `activeCommandConfigs` |
| onSettled 无 .catch()（unhandled rejection） | HIGH | 合并到 service 层统一监控，加 `.catch()` 错误处理 |
| trackTask stale Set 引用（竞态） | HIGH | `.then()` 内重新从 `trackingMap.get()` 查找 |
| lastCommandCode 永远为空 | HIGH | `onCommand` 中加 `updateStatus({ lastCommandCode })` |
| healthStatus/linkTestResult 不更新 | HIGH | handler 内根据 snapshot 写状态 |
| sendAckFrame 缺 frameReader guard | HIGH | 加 `frameReader.getFrame()` 前置检查 |
| onSettled 重复监控（handler + service） | HIGH | handler 只做 create/start，service 统一处理 settlement + ack + 计数 |
| sendAckFrame targetId 不检查空串 | LOW | `??` 改 `||` |

**Verify evidence:**
- `pnpm -C rewrite build`: 通过
- `vitest run src/features/command-ingress/`: 57 tests, 0 failures

**Open issues:** none
